### Verification 判定口徑

- 新 test fn 以 `(new)` 標示；執行時若改名，先同步卡片再跑。
- 零命中守衛必須區分 `rg` 退出碼 1 與 >1，使用模板 v2.8 的三分支 shell。
- 全量 nextest 只由 `Full-suite trigger`/收口門帶出，不計 Verification 分子。
- 所有 architecture guard 需斷言「provider module 無 SeaORM/HistoryManager/ref mutation import」與「coordinator 無 AgentKind/provider slug branch」，不能只做關鍵字存在性檢查。

### Task ACF-01：validated capture ingress contract

**Task type:** `implementation`

**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** `done` / `complete`

**Description:** 新建 `ai::capture` 的 validated ingress command，集中 frame cap、UTF-8/JSON/envelope validation、provider recognition/parser 與 canonical identity；現有 live runtime 立即改用它。

**Out of scope:** state transition 由 ACF-02；transcript 由 ACF-03；store I/O 由 ACF-04/05。

**Current evidence:**

| 事實 | 證據 |
|---|---|
| （歷史）decode/validate/provider parse 目前內嵌在 runtime | ACF-01 前 `hooks/runtime.rs:521-607`；現位於 `capture/ingress.rs` 的 `CaptureIngressCommand::from_stdin`（≈354）、`parse_capture_ingress_payload`（≈568）、`finish_capture_ingress`（≈647），runtime 入口僅 `hooks/runtime.rs::validate_capture_ingress_from_stdin`（≈336）呼叫 ingress service |
| reusable validator/parser contract 已存在 | `hooks/lifecycle.rs:192-233`；`hooks/provider.rs` 的 `HookProvider` trait（≈206-236；`parse_hook_event` ≈218、`recognizes_event` ≈234；ACF-18 於其前新增 `HookProviderIdentity`，原 ≈116-146） |
| compat architecture target 已存在且已註冊 | `tests/compat/agent_architecture_guard.rs`；`Cargo.toml:350-351`；`tests/INDEX.md:57`；`tests/compat/README.md:50` |

**Acceptance criteria:**

- [x] `CaptureIngressCommand` 只由 validated envelope + canonical `LifecycleEvent` 建構，欄位含 provider kind/source、scope input、event id、deadline。
- [x] 1 MiB、UTF-8、空 stdin、JSON、unsafe session id、path length 的錯誤分類保持原樣。
- [x] unknown provider event 保持 skip-and-log、零 catalog/checkpoint side effect。
- [x] provider parser 不取得 DB/ref/store handle。
- [x] runtime 原入口只呼叫 ingress service，不重複 decode/validation。
- [x] tracing 不含 raw stdin/prompt/tool payload。

**Verification:**

- [x] `cargo test --lib internal::ai::capture::ingress::tests` (nextest 12/12)
- [x] `cargo test --test agent_lifecycle_event_test capture_ingress_validation_matrix` (nextest 1/1; JSON scalar/string privacy and pipe/regular-file oversize wording)
- [x] `cargo test --test agent_hook_crash_test hook_handler_killed_mid_ingest_leaves_no_partial_write` (nextest 1/1)
- [x] `cargo test --test compat_agent_architecture_guard capture_ingress_has_no_store_dependency` (nextest 1/1; all provider parsers required and scanned)

**Verification update（2026-10-01 13:50 UTC）：** Claude R50/R51 findings (raw serde error value disclosure, incomplete dual-path oversize wording assertion, and parser guard silently skipping a provider directory without `parser.rs`) were fixed. R52 read-only whole-card review returned literal `VERDICT: PASS`. Focused nextest: ingress unit 12/12, validation matrix 1/1, killed-mid-ingest crash consistency 1/1, architecture boundary 1/1; `cargo +nightly fmt --all --check` and `libra diff --check` passed. The matrix now covers a top-level JSON string marker, a wrong-typed scalar, oversized pipe and regular-file stdin, stable `LBR-AGENT-008`, and no session/checkpoint side effects. Card lifecycle remains `in-progress` until whole-plan closeout gates by repository rule.

### Task ACF-02：typed lifecycle reducer 與 action plan

**Task type:** `implementation`

**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** `done` / `complete`

**Description:** 定義純 lifecycle reducer，以 typed state 與 canonical event 產生 deterministic next state/action plan，並以薄 adapter 取代 runtime 既有 state/event decision table。

**Out of scope:** 不改 SQL/ref/store orchestration；不啟用 async finalizer；provider-specific event taxonomy 仍由 provider plans。舊 `HookTarget::AiIntent` session JSON 的 UI `SessionPhase` (`transition_phase`) 仍保留 legacy projection；它不決定 durable `agent_session` state/checkpoint action，也不是本卡所指的平行 reducer。

**Current evidence:**

| 事實 | 證據 |
|---|---|

**Acceptance criteria:**

- [x] terminal action 只產生 `state='stopped' AND stopped_at IS NOT NULL` 的可消費終態。
- [x] live lifecycle reactivation action 產生非終態 state，不宣稱清空 `stopped_at`。
- [x] 每次 live lifecycle mutation action 嚴格增加 `sync_revision`。
- [x] 既有 explicit CLI resume 仍產生 `state='active' AND stopped_at IS NULL` 並增加 revision。
- [x] typed phase 對既有 DB state 有 total mapping。
- [x] 每個 `LifecycleEventKind` 對相同 input 產生 deterministic `(next_state, action_plan)`。
- [x] reducer 不依賴 filesystem、clock、DB、ref 或 provider identity，timestamp/deadline 全由 input 注入。
- [x] runtime 只以 reducer action plan 決定 lifecycle state/checkpoint action，不保留平行 event-kind decision table。

**Task type:** `implementation`

**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** `done` / `complete`

**Description:** 將 authorized read、preparer、hard cap、source digest、redaction、extraction/projection 與 partial reason 收斂為一個 snapshot service，live runtime 立即消費。

**Out of scope:** 不永久保存 raw；reasoning artifact 由 RG；OpenCode/Codex/Pi 原生 parser 由各 plan。

| CAP-07 計劃也準備重讀 provider raw | `plan-20260916.md` ADR-CAP-04 |
| ACF-03 現行實作：live/import 共用 snapshot boundary、隔離 descriptor helper、typed HMAC domain、partial 原因及 bypass guard | `capture/snapshot.rs` `CaptureSnapshotService`；`authorized_read.rs` `read_live_claude_source_until`；`agent_import.rs` `read_authorized_descriptor_until`；`command/agent/import.rs` `run_import_preparation_descriptor_helper_bounded`；`capture/key.rs` `derive_snapshot_content_commitment_in_scope_until`；`tests/compat/agent_architecture_guard.rs` `transcript_reads_only_through_snapshot_service` / downstream plan contract |
| focused card verification after helper env/cwd isolation and import deadline fix | snapshot/source/debug/extraction/partial/architecture 22/22；`agent_import_test` 48/48；import commitment classification 1/1；plan/downstream guard 2/2；docs contract 6/6；strict Clippy all targets/features、nightly fmt-check、`libra diff --check` pass |

**Acceptance criteria:**

- [x] `CaptureSnapshot` schema 含 authorized source identity/digest 與 typed completeness/partial reason。
- [x] durable transcript/projection/redaction report 只使用 typed redacted payload，snapshot 沒有 raw accessor、content-bearing `Serialize` 或 `Debug`。
- [x] service 只接受 `TranscriptSource`/`AgentSessionCtx`，不 reopen path。
- [x] source preparation helper 只以 held descriptor 與固定控制欄位工作；所有 source-preparation descriptor helper（live read／historical raw-read／import-preparation）清理環境並切至 neutral cwd，locator、provider/session id、path、metadata JSON 與 raw bytes 均不進 control wire/process environment。
- [x] `CaptureScope`、canonical root、storage policy 與 workspace fence 都在 helper read 前完成；新 durable import/source 或 snapshot-content identity 只可為 `source/hmac-v2/<64 lower-hex>`，subagent content identity 只可為其獨立 domain 的 `source/subagent-hmac-v2/<64-lower-hex>`，且不寫入 raw 或 unkeyed SHA-256。
- [x] 16 MiB cap 與合作式 preparer budget 在每個可取消讀取/投影階段前後都受 enforce；任何不可由 async timeout 搶占的阻塞 syscall 不得被描述成硬 deadline 保證，逾時/讀取未完成必須回 explicit partial 或 fail-closed outcome。
- [x] absent/untrusted/oversize/read-error/commitment-unavailable/deadline 各自映射到 explicit typed partial reason；extraction failure 也只能回 partial 或 fail-closed outcome，不以空物件冒充 complete。
- [x] live checkpoint 與後續 CAP-07 共用唯一 typed source digest/authorization seam；runtime 不直接呼叫 adapter `read_transcript` 或自行組合 transcript read/redaction/extraction chain。

### Task ACF-06：provider-neutral capture coordinator

**Task type:** `implementation`

**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** `done` / `complete`

**Description:** 組合 ingress、reducer、snapshot 與兩個 store 為 provider-neutral coordinator；所有 provider 共用同一 orchestration。**拆出 ACF-17、ACF-18、ACF-19、ACF-20**（2026-10-01 R66 P1 後使用者裁決做真實 runtime 薄化重構；按 G-03/G-04 粒度與 G-09 拆分，設計見 ADR-ACF-10）：ACF-17 抽取 legacy intent／scope binding 並先落地行為 oracle，ACF-19 抽取 live pipeline／checkpoint writers／read probes，ACF-18 交付 typed provider identity 與 live capture policy port，ACF-20 交付 export lease port 並驗收原 AC6「runtime 只做 IO framing／provider lookup／coordinator call／sanitized output」。

**Out of scope:** late finalization 由 ACF-07；historical import cutover 由 ACF-08；runtime 模組抽取由 ACF-17/19；provider-neutral live capture port 由 ACF-18；export lease port 與 runtime 薄化驗收由 ACF-20；provider-specific policy/normalizer 本身不在本卡。

|---|---|
| runtime（2026-10-01 為 12,393 行，約 5,000 production 行）仍同時處理 scope binding、legacy intent、live pipeline、checkpoint writers 與 provider 分支 | `wc -l src/internal/ai/hooks/runtime.rs`；R66 ACF06-G1；後續由 ACF-17/19/18/20 抽取（現位於 `hooks/intent.rs`、`capture/{scope_binding,live,live_pipeline,live_checkpoint}.rs` 與 `observed_agents/live_capture.rs`，見 ADR-ACF-10「Function→file（按卡）」；2026-10-02 `hooks/runtime.rs` 為 538 行） |
| provider plans 都把 runtime 列入 I 集 | `plan-20260902.md` OG cards；`plan-20260904.md` CX cards；`plan-20260905.md:378,553`；`plan-20260911.md:438` |

**Execution record（R66 後）：** ACF06-G3：`capture_coordinator_native_replay_matrix` 改為 fault stage × event kind 矩陣（初版 5 案例，leftover-marker 修正後新增第 6 案例 `turn-end-covered-marker-cleanup`）（TurnEnd post-registration fault、TurnEnd 由外部 live writer 持有 coverage、TurnEnd marker-cleanup fault、SessionEnd marker-cleanup fault、codex SubagentEnd post-registration fault），每案驗非零、content-free stderr、pending receipt、未 stopped，replay 後恰一 checkpoint、receipt complete、traces ref 不變。ACF06-G4：新增 `lifecycle_matrix_runs_through_fake_ports_and_production_handoff`（None／Committed／SubagentBoundary × terminal／nonterminal、Compaction→condensed，經 production reserve→claim→`into_coordinator().execute_preapplied`），改寫 `conflict_or_pending_cleanup_never_completes_terminal_receipt`；mutation 驗證會失敗。ACF06-G5：測試專用 entrypoints 改 `#[cfg(test)]`。ACF06-G6：新增 `hook_command_exit_and_output_contract_is_byte_stable` 釘 exit／stdout／stderr bytes。coordinator 19/19、相關整合 95/95，clippy／fmt 綠。G1／G2 依使用者裁決由 ACF-17/19/18/20 承接。另：G3 期間發現 coverage no-op settlement 不退役殘留 ordinary marker，另派修正（見修訂歷史）。

### Task ACF-07：合作式 deadline pending finalizer policy

**Task type:** `implementation`

**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** `done` / `complete`

**Description:** 在 coordinator 上加入 provider-neutral 的合作式 deadline finalize policy，統一 terminal receipt 的 bounded replay 決策。拆出 ACF-10 durable artifact、ACF-11 child owner；後續再拆出 ACF-14 key owner、ACF-12 executor/doctor、ACF-13 live handoff。

**Out of scope:** durable snapshot/index 由 ACF-10；doctor 由 ACF-12、SessionStart 由 ACF-13、process owner 由 ACF-11。不替 provider 決定 hook budget；raw spool/path receipt/常駐 daemon 為永久非目標。不承諾任意 blocking syscall 的硬 deadline。

**Current evidence:**

| 事實 | 證據 |
|---|---|
| Codex/Claude/OpenCode plans 各自規劃 timeout/finalize/ledger | `plan-20260904.md` CX-02/CX-17；`plan-20260905.md` CC-02；`plan-20260902.md` OG-02/07 |
| existing marker/repair primitives 可承載 replay | `traces.rs` 的 `TracesInflightMarker`（≈847）、`retire_stale_traces_inflight_marker`（≈1080）、`terminal_attempt_marker_status`（≈1157）；`command/agent/doctor.rs` 的 `FinalizerRepairKind`（≈447）、`diagnose_pending_finalizer`（≈466） |
| Entire optional `SessionEndBudgeter` 與 session phase/lock 將 deadline 從 provider 注入 strategy | Entire `agent/agent.go:447-470`；`strategy/session_state.go:31-64` |

**Acceptance criteria:**

- [x] policy 不含 provider identity。
- [x] terminal completion 必須消費 durable checkpoint proof。
- [x] pending_source 不產生自動 replay authorization。
- [x] replay 不重設 original policy、generation 或 stable key。
- [x] deferrable 重試遵守首次 attempt 起 ≤5 次/30 分鐘上限。
- [x] synchronous expired receipt 只回 incomplete/repair-required。

**Verification:**

- [x] `source .env.test && cargo test --lib internal::ai::capture::finalizer::tests`
- [x] `source .env.test && cargo test --lib internal::ai::capture::catalog::tests`
- [x] `source .env.test && cargo test --test agent_lifecycle_event_test stale_finalizer_cannot_overwrite_takeover` `(new)`
- [x] `source .env.test && cargo test --test compat_agent_architecture_guard provider_plans_consume_common_finalizer_policy` `(new)`

**Full-suite trigger:** `T-1: reliability-critical terminal state/recovery path`

**Dependencies:** `ACF-06`

**Deliverables:** N/A

**Implementation write set:** `src/internal/ai/capture/{finalizer,coordinator,catalog}.rs`、`src/internal/ai/hooks/runtime.rs`、`tests/agent_lifecycle_event_test.rs`、`tests/compat/agent_architecture_guard.rs`、`docs/development/tracing/agent.md`；**收口寫集對帳補登（2026-10-02 09:00:36 UTC）：** `src/internal/db.rs`（deadline-bounded hook DB open）。補登後 3 生產目錄／5 生產檔

**Rollback mode:** `forward-only`

**Migration and rollback:** 無 schema；receipt wire 保持原形，新增 private artifact 的 recovery forward-only。不能假設舊 doctor 可理解新版 artifact；保留 evidence 並升回新版 repair，不以 downgrade 刪除資料。

**Security and privacy:** receipt/diagnostic 只含 ids、digest、stage、count、sanitized error class。

**Version increment:** `N/A（使用者明確禁止 bump）`

### Task ACF-08：historical import cutover

**Task type:** `implementation`

**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** `done` / `complete`

**Description:** 將 historical import 切到已由 live path 驗證的 snapshot/catalog/checkpoint/coordinator services，移除 import 自有的 source/store orchestration；descriptor-pinned legacy reader 僅保留為一次受限的匯入輸入取得邊界，讀得 bytes 後立即交給 shared snapshot，不構成另一條 snapshot/catalog/checkpoint I/O 路徑。

**Out of scope:** 不實作 provider-specific parser/reasoning/export；不修改 CAP HTTP protocol；不移除 legacy readers；不在本卡解除下游 gate（由 ACF-09）。

**Current evidence:**

| 事實 | 證據 |
|---|---|
| （歷史）import 曾自行做 source read/normalization/checkpoint/catalog transaction；現經 `ImportSnapshotPort`／`CaptureSnapshotService`、`CaptureCatalogStore::{prepare_import_session, apply_import_session_lifecycle, migrate_import_session_ownership, discard_unprogressed_import_session}`、`ImportedCheckpointWriter`（`capture/checkpoint.rs`，ACF-05 擁有）與 coverage_gate claim helpers | `agent_import.rs`；`capture/catalog.rs`；`capture/checkpoint.rs` |
| live 與 import 共用 primitives 但非 service | `TranscriptSource`、`CaptureScope`、`TracesTxnExtra` 各自被兩條路徑直接調用 |
| import session lifecycle SQL 的真實 writer 已移至 `CaptureCatalogStore::apply_import_session_lifecycle`；import 的 `agent_coverage_claim` 轉移改由 `coverage_gate::{abandon_reserved_turn_claims_with_conn, renew_import_turn_claim_leases_with_conn, bind_import_turn_claim_attempt_with_conn}` 在 import transaction 內執行 | `capture/catalog.rs`；`coverage_gate.rs` |
| 保留 reader 的邊界 | historical export/held-FD acquisition 是既有相容輸入邊界：只作一次 bounded read、不得 reopen 或再讀全文，後續一律 handoff 至 shared snapshot；由 `compat_agent_architecture_guard` 鎖定 |

**Current evidence:** bounded artifact recovery已由 `command/agent/doctor.rs::run_pending_artifact_worker` 限定為最多5筆/2秒；`capture/worker.rs` 以 private no-follow lock file 的 OS advisory `File::try_lock` 單倉互斥、固定 hidden token/null stdio/verified cwd/Unix setsid，`cli.rs` raw-argv最前置 dispatch，clean env且無等待。SessionStart只查indexed `LIMIT 1` hint再best-effort spawn。實際source-free detached replay及lock/path拒絕測試已通過，但並發child/殺死worker後OS釋鎖、pipe/FD隔離與非Unix平台證據尚待專項補齊；不提前接受本卡。（開工時快照；並發 child、殺 worker 釋鎖與 pipe/FD 隔離其後已由 `capture/worker.rs` tests 覆蓋，見 R66 後執行記錄。）

**Execution checkpoint（2026-10-01；本輪擴展復審修訂）：** worker 以 indexed pending headers 為唯一自動候選來源，不再以 doctor 的全量 session keyset scan 作自動執行前置；每次最多解碼五個候選，逐條以 HMAC alias、當前 catalog revision、完整 pending receipt/finalizer fence 做 PK point lookup。失效、superseded、quarantined 或耗盡 budget 的 header 會轉入保留證據的 artifact quarantine，避免卡住後續候選；quarantine 失敗有 content-free warning 並停止本批。真實 binary source-free recovery 測試額外插入 520 條無關 scoped session（超過 doctor 的 512 session diagnostic cap）後仍完成；SessionStart 父 hook <2s，並由 stdout/stderr piped harness 驗證不等待 detached child。worker lock 單元測試具名為 `concurrent_recovery_triggers_have_one_active_worker`、`killed_worker_process_releases_os_advisory_lock`；hidden-entry guard 與 bad-argv-before-DB integration 繼續作為隔離證據。AC/驗收仍待最終 gate/review，不提前標 done。

- [x] parent 不 wait child。
- [x] OS advisory lock 保證每 repo 最多一個 active worker。
- [x] hidden child dispatch 跳過 startup recovery/config schema/auto-upgrade side effects。

**Performance budget:** queue `LIMIT 5`、2 秒 cooperative batch；doctor cold scan 有界 keyset pagination，壞 ledger 記 manual note 後繼續；不 scan refs 作 pending discovery。

## 測試矩陣

| 類別 | 必須覆蓋 | Target / command |
|---|---|---|
| 單元 | ingress、phase/action、snapshot、store result、deadline/finalizer | `cargo test --lib internal::ai::capture`
| live integration | 全 lifecycle matrix、dedup、unknown、subagent、host response | `cargo test --test agent_lifecycle_event_test`
| import integration | consent/identity/lease、same snapshot/store、failure recovery | `cargo test --test agent_import_test`
| checkpoint | redaction type、content hash、CAS/catalog atomicity | `cargo test --test agent_checkpoint_redaction_test` |
| crash/recovery | 每個 durable fault point、takeover、stale marker | `cargo test --test agent_hook_crash_test`；`agent_doctor_repair_test` |
| durable artifact | ACF-10 MAC、bounds、erase、marker expiry/GC、source removal | `source .env.test && cargo test --lib internal::ai::capture::pending::tests`；ACF-10 指定私有 port tests |
| private session alias | ACF-15 indexed/MAC/scoped alias、local-only／GC exclusion、atomic mint | ACF-15 七個指定門；private provider/native PK、VACUUM fixture |
| portable payload | ACF-16 native control identity exclusion、sealed/MAC prerequisite、redacted rehydration／coverage | ACF-16 七個指定門；normal/recovered sidecar equivalence |
| shared private key | ACF-14 single owner、existing domain compatibility、read-only verify | `source .env.test && cargo test --lib internal::ai::capture::key::tests`；既有 runtime staging crash tests |
| one-shot worker | ACF-11 lock/crash/descriptor ownership | `source .env.test && cargo test --lib internal::ai::capture::worker::tests`；ACF-11 指定集成 tests |
| bounded recovery | ACF-12 indexed queue、expiry/manual、resume/source/status | `source .env.test && cargo test --lib internal::ai::capture::recovery::tests`；ACF-12 指定 tests |
| baseline lock | FIX-ACF-01 default-before-resource ordering | `source .env.test && TMPDIR=/Volumes/Sky/tmp cargo test --lib -- test_ensure_vault_ssh_tmp_dir_uses_home_directory pkt_line_empty_discovery_http_tail_maps_net_002 --test-threads=2`；`compat_serial_registry` |
| live handoff | ACF-13 durable snapshot、claim retention、early hint | `source .env.test && cargo test --test agent_hook_crash_test`；ACF-13 指定 tests |
| runtime extraction | ACF-17/19 pure-move byte compatibility、moved tests、re-pointed guards | `source .env.test && cargo test --test agent_lifecycle_event_test hook_public_contract_byte_compat_matrix`；ACF-17/19 指定 tests |
| live capture port | ACF-18/20 typed identity、single lookup、provider ports、lease exits、thin runtime | `source .env.test && cargo test --lib internal::ai::observed_agents::live_capture::tests`；ACF-18/20 指定 guards |
| observability | content-free stage/outcome spans | `cargo test --test agent_hook_span_test` |
| compatibility | layering、provider-neutral、docs/plan mirrors | `cargo test --test compat_agent_architecture_guard`；`cargo test --test session_capture_docs_contract` |
| full | ER-13/14 完整 suite | `source .env.test && source .env.live-test && cargo nextest run --all --no-fail-fast --retries 2` |

## 里程碑驗收與回滾

FIX-ACF-01 為 ACF-09 最終 full-suite/handoff 前置；僅測試鎖修復，outgoing DEP 仍不解鎖。

| 里程碑 | 完成條件 | 發布/證據 | 回滾或前滾 |
|---|---|---|---|
| M0 | Entire/Libra pin、計劃設計 review、DEP mirrors | plan commit | 文件 revert |
| M1 | ACF-01..03 complete | 本地 focused gates 與 direct-main closeout evidence；不發版 | 各卡 revert；無 schema/data rewrite |
| M2 | ACF-04..06 complete | store/coordinator local gates + architecture guard；不發版 | 各卡 reverse order revert |
| M3 | ACF-07/14/15/16/10/12/11/13/17/19/18/20/08 complete、全量本地 suite 綠；ACF-09 handoff complete | finalizer/key/alias/portable-payload/artifact/recovery/worker/live/import local gates、signed+DCO direct-main commit 與 `origin/main` push；不 bump/不產生 release artifact | pending evidence forward-fix；key/import可revert；handoff依消費狀態 revert/handback |

### 故障恢復矩陣

| 故障點 | 可接受殘留 | 恢復動作 | 禁止結果 |
|---|---|---|---|
| ingress validation | sanitized error/span | 修 payload/adapter 後重送 | partial DB/ref write |
| catalog transaction | rollback 或 idempotency receipt | same replay key 重送 | false checkpoint row |
| object built、ref CAS 前 | unreachable objects/marker | bounded retry/GC | catalog success |
| ref/catalog transaction | 全部 rollback | same generation retry | ref-only or row-only success |
| deadline exhausted | pending receipt + marker | bounded finalizer replay | stopped/complete without checkpoint |
| source acquisition blocks before safe snapshot | pending evidence only if receipt write completes; otherwise nonterminal/error | provider replays event with newly authorized FD, or doctor reports `capture_incomplete` | path-based reopen, synthetic empty snapshot, terminal success |
| repeated SessionStart / sweeper crash | existing receipt + repo OS advisory lock | same stable key；自動 within cap，過期一次 explicit audited repair | duplicate checkpoint, parallel sweepers, resident daemon |
| next SessionStart after 30 minutes | retained quarantined snapshot | single audited operator attempt if current fences match，或 explicit session erase | reset original counters, silent discard, stale terminal |
| stale finalizer takeover | old receipt/marker可診斷 | generation compare 拒絕 old writer | delete/overwrite new state |
| import crash | existing lease/claim/marker | existing recovery + same identity replay | second semantic checkpoint |

## 風險登記

| 風險 | 影響 | 緩解 | 任務 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 抽象層只搬碼未降耦合 | 高：provider plans仍改 shared runtime | architecture guard（ACF-20 macro-aware provider-literal 掃描＋runtime inventory/行數上限）+ provider plan mirrors | ACF-06/17/19/18/20/08 |
| store façade弱化原子性 | P0 data/ref divergence | typed request + fault injection + same txn | ACF-04/05 |
| raw bytes跨層洩漏 | P0 secrets/PII | non-Serialize raw、RedactedBytes sink、canary | ACF-03/05 |
| blocking syscall 越過 cooperative budget | hook process 可能晚於預期返回；host callback 可能失敗 | bounded helper stages、nonterminal state、content-free diagnostic、明確記錄不提供 hard wall-clock guarantee | ACF-03/07 |
| snapshot 尚未 durable 時 source 不可再取得 | 該次捕獲無法自動重建 | 不持久化 path/raw；provider replay with authorized FD，否則 `capture_incomplete` / `repair-required` | ACF-03/07 |
| deadline誤標完成 | P1 silent capture loss | pending receipt/replay/doctor evidence | ACF-07/10/12/13 |
| pending artifact 遺失／改寫 | P1 incomplete/corrupt replay | indexed SQLite durability、domain-separated MAC、erase transaction | ACF-10 |
| native PK 進 opaque binding／sidecar | P1 capture 無法接線或敏感 locator 複製 | private MAC alias registry、portable projection／原 catalog PK memory-only bridge | ACF-15/16/10 |
| worker ownership／resume drift | P1 leaked host handles/stale terminal | advisory child lock、null stdio/CLOEXEC、generation fence、manual diagnostics | ACF-11/12/13 |
| key loader 分叉／遺失 key 靜默重建 | P1 integrity 不可驗／新增不可信 key | 單一 owner、既有 domain byte-compatibility、pending verify readonly | ACF-14 |
| 舊 plan 形成循環依賴 | P1 無法開工 | 只允許 ACF→provider；ACF 不消費 RG/provider卡；末端 handoff 才放行 | ACF-09 |
| monolith cutover regression | 高：所有 provider capture | per-card existing regression、ACF-17 byte-compat golden matrix 與 move audit（純移動 token 相同）、ACF-18 port-decision matrix + full suite | ACF-06/17/19/18/20/08 |
| per-checkpoint refs延後造成 contention | 中 | store API不曝露 branch topology；DEFER-ACF-01 | ACF-05 |

## 性能與容量摘要

| 操作 | 單次成本 | 累積成本 | 預算/上限 | 驗證 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| ingress | O(frame) | 每 hook 一次 | frame ≤1 MiB；≤1 clone | ACF-01 tests |
| reducer | O(1)+bounded arrays | 每 event | arrays 維持 200/400 cap | ACF-02 matrix |
| snapshot | O(transcript) | checkpoint boundary | ≤16 MiB；一次 read/redact/project | ACF-03 tests |
| catalog | indexed O(log n) | 每 event/action | 不增 unbounded scan；round trip ≤現行+1 | ACF-04 tests |
| checkpoint | O(snapshot + object tree) | boundary events | CAS/retry/objects ≤現行 | ACF-05 metrics |
| finalize/recovery | cooperative deadline；one-shot sweep | terminal event / later SessionStart / doctor --repair | per repo ≤1 worker；每批 ≤5 receipts；≤5 replay attempts/receipt/30 min；無熱路徑 ref walk | ACF-07/11/12/13 tests |
| alias registry | indexed LIMIT1 resolve / LIMIT17 reverse probe | sensitive local catalog association only | ≤16 rows/repo、≤8KiB/row；不進 GC/cloud | ACF-15 tests |
| portable projection | bounded typed sidecars | exact redacted transcript + safe provenance | 原24MiB/4MiB caps、JSON depth≤64 | ACF-16 tests |
| pending artifact | indexed SQLite header/chunks | only redacted durable payload | ≤16/repo、≤64 MiB/artifact、≤128 chunks | ACF-10 tests |
| import batch | O(total authorized bytes) | command | total 64 MiB、120 s、per source 16 MiB | ACF-08 tests |

## 非目標與延後項

| ID | 延後內容 | 原因 | 重啟條件 | 承接位置 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| DEFER-ACF-01 | `refs/libra/traces` → one-ref-per-checkpoint backend | 需 ref discovery/push/fetch/GC/migration 完整協議，非本輪分層前置 | ACF store façade穩定且量測證明 single-ref contention | 新日期計劃 |
| DEFER-ACF-02 | raw native transcript durable archive/resume | 與 Libra redaction/consent/cloud policy衝突 | 有明確加密、consent、erase、cloud與舊 reader契約 | security/privacy RFC + plan |
| DEFER-ACF-03 | attribution/commit trailer/agent-owned line tracking | 是 commit association 行為，不是 capture foundation | CX-11/23/27 收口後另評估 | plan-20260904 或新計劃 |
| DEFER-ACF-04 | 移除 `AgentStability`/preview policy | Entire latest 已移除 core preview，但 Libra preview仍是 CLI/DB roster相容面 | roster/schema migration有獨立需求 | 新日期計劃 |
| DEFER-ACF-05 | TurnEnd/nonterminal 自主補齊 | 使用者選擇方案 1，terminal sweeper 僅承諾 SessionEnd；provider 不保證重投，接受 turn-level checkpoint 可能缺失 | 使用者另行明確要求，並完成 event/claim/locator-free durability 與 replay safety 獨立設計 | 新日期計劃；本輪不實作 |
| DEFER-ACF-06 | `capture/snapshot.rs::capture_live_until` 與 `capture/extraction.rs` 的既有 `AgentKind` dispatch 移到 `LiveCaptureProvider`／`ObservedAgent` capability | 移除會改變 Codex/OpenCode `partial_reason` 與 extraction wire code，違反 ACF-17..20 byte 相容；ACF-20 以 non-test 計數 ratchet（1／19）凍結只可下降 | 下游 provider 計劃需改 snapshot／extraction 行為，或有獨立卡定義 `partial_reason`/wire 相容遷移 | 新日期計劃（provider plan owner） |
| DEFER-ACF-07 | `observed_agents/live_capture.rs` → `subagent_content`（含 sea_orm 依賴）邊；Claude child discovery 移入 `observed_agents/builtin/claude_code.rs` | 本輪以三名稱 allowlist 隔離；搬移需改 subagent_content 與 import 呼叫方，超出 ACF-18 粒度 | 下游 Claude 計劃（plan-20260905）觸及 subagent discovery 或 guard 發現 allowlist 外名稱 | plan-20260905 或新日期計劃 |
| DEFER-ACF-08 | 各 provider 的 `LiveCaptureProvider` 實作移到各自 builtin adapter 檔，避免 provider 計劃共寫 `observed_agents/live_capture.rs` | ACF-18 為守 G-04 落點上限集中於一檔；guard 已允許 `observed_agents/**` 任意位置 | 第一個需要修改 live capture 實作的 provider 計劃開工前 | 該 provider 計劃首張 production 卡 |

## 收口寫集對帳（2026-10-02 09:00:36 UTC）

最終 review（R84）以工作樹對照全部卡 `Implementation write set` 聯集，發現 41 個屬本計劃的生產檔（另含測試／文檔／registry）未在任何卡預先登記；多數來自 2026-09-28..30 公開契約審計與早期實作期，或其後 fix 的寫集外觸及（ER-10「先改寫集」的歷史偏差）。下表逐檔補登歸屬，各卡 `Implementation write set` 末尾以「收口寫集對帳補登」同步，`Granularity` 與粒度審計表按補登後重算；超出 G-04 M 的 ACF-01、ACF-03、ACF-06（R86–R88 補登後 6/11，另 AC 9/8 超 G-03）、ACF-08（四項核准公開收緊）、ACF-12（R86 補登 `src/cli.rs` 後 5/11），以及 AC 9/8 的 ACF-12，依模板「模板版本與迁移政策」登記於修訂歷史例外列。`libra diff` 對超過 10,000 行的檔案只輸出 `<LargeFile>`（numstat 0/0），其實際差異以 `libra show HEAD:<path>` 比對（`subagent_content.rs` +4784/−981、`client_storage.rs` +399/−34、`d1_client.rs` +203/−9、`merge.rs` 屬 T-6）。

| 歸屬 | 生產檔（補登） | 變更摘要 |
|---|---|---|
| ACF-01 | `capture/runtime_scope.rs`（新）；`hooks/providers/{claude,codex,gemini,opencode}/{mod,parser}.rs`；`hooks/providers/{claude,codex}/settings.rs`、`hooks/setup.rs` | ingress 攜帶不透明 `CaptureRuntimeScope`；移除 `lifecycle_fallback_events` 與 fallback 常數；核准例外 #1/#2 的受管 hook grammar、budget/timeout 對、`statusMessage` ownership 與精確匹配 refresh/disable |
| ACF-02 | `hooks/session_capture.rs`（刪除） | 舊 event-kind 決策表由純 reducer 取代 |
| ACF-03 | `main.rs`；`command/cloud.rs`、`command/cloud/{agent_capture,object_format,restore,sync}.rs`；`command/agent/list.rs`；`internal/config_ownership.rs`；`ai/subagent_content.rs`；`observed_agents/{coverage,extract,opencode_export,redaction,trust}.rs`；`observed_agents/builtin/{claude_code,mod}.rs`；`utils/d1_client.rs` | 有界 helper（authorized-read v2、import descriptor、subagent projection、extraction）；snapshot／redaction／extraction 上限；OpenCode exporter FD pin 與 macOS fail-closed；subagent `source/subagent-hmac-v2`；cloud D1 錯誤不回顯遠端文本、遠端 repo id UUID 驗證、tombstone 不再輸出 legacy fingerprint；核准的 `cloud_sync.agent_capture.warning` 固定事件；R85 前滾：受信 exporter 串流 hash 與 Linux sealed copy 不設固定大小上限（以 descriptor 自身 stat 長度為界，撤回會拒絕並撤銷真實 ~171 MiB OpenCode 單檔信任的 64 MiB 上限）、cloud sync 失敗只由 `sync::mirror_agent_capture` 輸出一次固定具體原因並以 `agent capture mirror failed: <reason>`（`LBR-CONFLICT-002`）結束、restore 失敗恢復 `agent capture restore failed: <reason>`（`LBR-NET-002`）、`agent import` 恢復 `sha256:<12 hex>` item id 與可操作的 selector 錯誤訊息 |
| ACF-05 | `utils/client_storage.rs` | traces CAS 交易內的 object-index upsert |
| ACF-06 | `command/hooks.rs`、`cli.rs`（agent hook 早期分派與 deadline）；R86／R88：`capture/{scope_binding,live_pipeline}.rs`；R87：`internal/operation/restore.rs`、`command/op.rs` | Codex terminal disposition、`map_capture_ingest_error`（`LBR-AGENT-008`、倉庫外 `LBR-REPO-001`、非 Unix `LBR-UNSUPPORTED-001`）、hidden `--capture-budget-ms`；R87：hook 早期分派繞過 operation boundary 後，`op undo`／`redo`／`revert`／`restore` 保留 `traces`／`agent-traces`／`intent`／`libra/intent` 現值 |
| ACF-07 | `internal/db.rs` | deadline-bounded hook DB open，tracing 不含 db 路徑 |
| ACF-08 | `command/agent/{capture_source,session,checkpoint}.rs` | 核准例外 #3/#4 與 2026-10-02 收緊：extract-transcript 自 durable identity 重導出且 JSON 無 `source_path`、`checkpoint show` 固定安全摘要、rewind 只恢復工作樹 |
| ACF-12 | `hooks/providers/opencode/settings.rs`、`utils/object.rs`；R86：`cli.rs`（`agent doctor` preflight arm） | doctor status probe 不輸出路徑；doctor 唯讀 no-follow 物件讀取與有界驗證 |
| ACF-20 | `ai/export_job.rs` | `ExportJobLeaseStore` 背後的 deadline-bounded observe/advance/release 與 workspace fence |
| T-6（全量收口修復，非 ACF 卡） | `observed_agents/builtin/stable_promoted.rs`、`internal/operation/snapshot.rs`、`lib.rs`；`internal/{auth,config}.rs`；`internal/{layer,sparse}/mod.rs`、`internal/worktree_scope.rs`、`internal/worktree_scope/context.rs`、`internal/protocol/local_client.rs`、`command/{show,rename_detect,merge}.rs`、`command/worktree/operations.rs`、`utils/{test,output}.rs`、`internal/upgrade/settings.rs`、`ai/sandbox/mod.rs`；`fetch.rs`（FIX-ACF-01） | debug `LIBRA_TEST_*` env failpoint 改為 `cfg(test)` 型別化控制；auth 唯讀查找不建立 global config DB；cwd／env／`service`／`warning_tracker` serial lanes 與 `cwd_lock_guard` 協調；`op_undo_redo` 測試宿主判定；mock HTTP 完整讀取請求 |

測試／文檔補登：ACF-01、03、05、08、12 的測試與文檔已列於各卡補登句；T-6 另含 `tests/command/{add,commit,hash_object,agent_clean,agent_push,service,worktree,mega2_browser_mutate}_test.rs`、`tests/network_remotes_test.rs`、`tests/compat/serial_registry.rs`、`tests/{SERIAL_REGISTRY,SRC_SERIAL_REGISTRY,SRC_SERIAL_CENSUS}.tsv`、`.config/nextest.toml`（generator）、`docs/development/commands/add.md` 與 `docs/development/plan/plan-20260918.md`（退役的 `LIBRA_TEST_OBJECT_INDEX_UPDATE_DELAY_MS` 註記）。ACF-19 另補 `tests/agent_checkpoint_span_test.rs`，ACF-08 另補 `docs/development/tracing/plan.md`，ACF-03 另補 `docs/development/plan/plan-20260830.md`。
