- [x] 版本面與 `Cargo.lock` 均未變更；確認 `commit.gpgSign`（或 `vault.signing`）後以 `libra commit -s` 建立提交。提交後以 raw object 驗證而非 `libra cat-file -p`：`COMMIT_SHA="$(libra rev-parse HEAD)"; printf '%s\n' "$COMMIT_SHA" | libra cat-file --batch | rg 'gpgsig|Signed-off-by'` 必須同時命中簽名與 DCO trailer，才可 `libra push origin main`。不建立 PR、不產生 release/CDN/CI/CodeQL 聲稱。
- [x] 無新 SQLite schema；若產生 migration，已先修訂計劃、GC-13/old-new/down/fault gates 全部完成。
- [x] 必要的 docs/compat/error-code/test-index 更新已完成；判定 N/A 的 public surface 仍以 source/測試證據記錄。
- [x] 每張卡的 `Rollback mode` 已以 fault/revert/forward-replay 證據驗證，或記錄無法實測的具體原因；ACF-07 pending evidence 與 ACF-09 consumed-handoff handback 必須有專屬證據。
- [x] raw transcript、token、prompt、tool payload 未出現在普通 metadata/log/error/trace；opaque reasoning 邊界未被繞過。
- [x] live/import 共用 foundation，provider/RPC adapter 無直接 SQL/ref writes，coordinator 無 provider-specific branch。
- [x] 0902/0904/0905/0911 production 卡只在 ACF-09 complete 後解除其 `DEP-ACF-MIRROR` gate；0916 CAP-01..03 仍僅可 zero-raw-persistence 重核，CAP-04、CAP-05、CAP-06、CAP-07（即 CAP-04..07，含 CAP-07）即使 ACF-09 complete 也持續 `blocked`，直到獨立 security/privacy RFC 與新卡驗收完成。ACF-09 對 CAP consumer 只是必要而非充分條件；`plan-status.md`/`plan-long.md` 同步。
- [x] 驗收證據均寫入本計劃文件（模板 v2.12）；`/Volumes/Sky/tmp/libra-acf1?-evidence`／`libra-acf20-evidence` 等外部 scratch 證據包與 junit 檔僅為非權威輔助，所有承重數據已內嵌於執行記錄與附錄 A。
- [x] Claude 最終 review 字面 `VERDICT: PASS`，P0/P1/P2 全部關閉；修訂歷史與 review log 已回填。

## 附錄 A：ACF-17／ACF-19 move-audit 工具與結果（ADR-ACF-10 move-audit method）

依 ADR-ACF-10，move-audit 腳本原始碼與逐 item 表完整記錄於此；耐久證據包另存於 `/Volumes/Sky/tmp/libra-acf17-evidence` 與 `/Volumes/Sky/tmp/libra-acf19-evidence`（各含 SHA256SUMS）。工具為 scratchpad 外的獨立 crate（`[workspace]` 隔離，`cargo build --offline`），不進入本 repo 建置。

<details><summary>A.1 Cargo.toml（sha256 a9318eeeb5346f630effc3116998c39924d094ecb0f1634e46ea984c1f743036，ACF-17／ACF-19 共用）</summary>

````toml
[package]
name = "moveaudit"
version = "0.1.0"
edition = "2021"

[dependencies]
syn = { version = "=2.0.117", features = ["full", "visit", "visit-mut", "extra-traits", "printing"] }
proc-macro2 = { version = "=1.0.106", features = ["span-locations"] }
quote = "=1.0.45"
sha2 = "=0.10.9"

[workspace]
````

</details>

<details><summary>A.2 ACF-17 版 src/main.rs（sha256 3410bdc26e9528da0e3ba2c1c81b6e0b0360314e6c9e6badeaa00a9efd9129dd，682 行）</summary>

````rust
//! ACF-17 scratch move-audit tool (ADR-ACF-10 "Move-audit method").
//!
//! Usage:
//!   moveaudit list <file.rs>
//!   moveaudit audit <spec.txt>
//!
//! `list` prints every item (recursing into inline modules) as
//! `<module-path>::<key>\t<first_line>-<last_line>\t<cfg_test>`.
//!
//! `audit` reads a spec of lines:
//!   pre <path>                     -- the pre-move source (acf17-runtime-pre.rs)
//!   post <label> <path>            -- every post-move file that may hold a moved item
//!   base <label> <path>            -- the pre-move snapshot of an already existing post file
//!   move <pre-key> <label>         -- expected destination label of a moved item
//! and prints a per-item table plus the "found in more than one location"
//! and "unclassifiable" lists. The audit fails (exit 1) if either list is
//! non-empty, or if any spec'd destination does not match.
//!
//! Allowed difference categories (ADR-ACF-10 pure-move (1)-(4)):
//!   (1) path prefix: insertion/removal of leading `ident ::` path segments
//!   (2) visibility: any change of an item/field `Visibility`
//!   (3) intra-doc link: a `#[doc]` string that differs only after link
//!       normalisation (`[`a::b`]` / `[x](a::b)` -> last segment, de-link)
//!   (4) test_support seam path: a path-prefix difference whose path names
//!       a `test_support` segment
//! Category 0 = byte-identical token stream.

use std::{
    collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet},
    fs,
    process::ExitCode,
};

use proc_macro2::{Delimiter, TokenStream, TokenTree};
use quote::ToTokens;
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use syn::{visit_mut::VisitMut, Attribute, Item};

#[derive(Clone)]
struct Located {
    module: String,
    key: String,
    first: usize,
    last: usize,
    cfg_test: bool,
    item: Item,
}

fn is_cfg_test(attrs: &[Attribute]) -> bool {
    attrs.iter().any(|a| {
        a.path().is_ident("cfg") && a.meta.to_token_stream().to_string().replace(' ', "") == "cfg(test)"
    })
}

fn item_attrs(item: &Item) -> &[Attribute] {
    match item {
        Item::Const(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Enum(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::ExternCrate(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Fn(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::ForeignMod(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Impl(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Macro(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Mod(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Static(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Struct(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Trait(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::TraitAlias(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Type(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Union(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Use(i) => &i.attrs,
        _ => &[],
    }
}

fn type_key(ty: &syn::Type) -> String {
    ty.to_token_stream().to_string().replace(' ', "")
}

fn macro_statics(tokens: &TokenStream) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut names = Vec::new();
    let toks: Vec<TokenTree> = tokens.clone().into_iter().collect();
    for window in toks.windows(2) {
        if let (TokenTree::Ident(a), TokenTree::Ident(b)) = (&window[0], &window[1]) {
            if a == "static" {
                names.push(b.to_string());
            }
        }
    }
    names
}

fn item_key(item: &Item) -> String {
    match item {
        Item::Const(i) => format!("const {}", i.ident),
        Item::Enum(i) => format!("enum {}", i.ident),
        Item::Fn(i) => format!("fn {}", i.sig.ident),
        Item::Impl(i) => match &i.trait_ {
            Some((_, path, _)) => format!(
                "impl {} for {}",
                path.to_token_stream().to_string().replace(' ', ""),
                type_key(&i.self_ty)
            ),
            None => format!("impl {}", type_key(&i.self_ty)),
        },
        Item::Macro(i) => {
            let path = i.mac.path.to_token_stream().to_string().replace(' ', "");
            match &i.ident {
                Some(ident) => format!("macro_rules {ident}"),
                None => format!("macro {}!{{{}}}", path, macro_statics(&i.mac.tokens).join(",")),
            }
        }
        Item::Mod(i) => format!("mod {}", i.ident),
        Item::Static(i) => format!("static {}", i.ident),
        Item::Struct(i) => format!("struct {}", i.ident),
        Item::Trait(i) => format!("trait {}", i.ident),
        Item::Type(i) => format!("type {}", i.ident),
        Item::Union(i) => format!("union {}", i.ident),
        Item::Use(i) => format!("use {}", i.tree.to_token_stream().to_string().replace(' ', "")),
        other => format!("other {}", other.to_token_stream().to_string().chars().take(40).collect::<String>()),
    }
}

fn span_lines(item: &Item) -> (usize, usize) {
    use syn::spanned::Spanned;
    let mut first = item.span().start().line;
    for attr in item_attrs(item) {
        first = first.min(attr.span().start().line);
    }
    (first, item.span().end().line)
}

fn collect(items: &[Item], module: &str, inherited_test: bool, out: &mut Vec<Located>) {
    for item in items {
        let cfg_test = inherited_test || is_cfg_test(item_attrs(item));
        let key = item_key(item);
        let (first, last) = span_lines(item);
        out.push(Located {
            module: module.to_string(),
            key: key.clone(),
            first,
            last,
            cfg_test,
            item: item.clone(),
        });
        if let Item::Mod(m) = item {
            if let Some((_, nested)) = &m.content {
                let child = if module.is_empty() {
                    m.ident.to_string()
                } else {
                    format!("{module}::{}", m.ident)
                };
                collect(nested, &child, cfg_test, out);
            }
        }
    }
}

fn parse(path: &str) -> (Vec<Located>, String) {
    let text = fs::read_to_string(path).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("read {path}: {e}"));
    let file = syn::parse_file(&text).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("parse {path}: {e}"));
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    collect(&file.items, "", false, &mut out);
    (out, sha256_hex(text.as_bytes()))
}

fn sha256_hex(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    let digest = Sha256::digest(bytes);
    digest.iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect()
}

fn full_key(l: &Located) -> String {
    if l.module.is_empty() {
        l.key.clone()
    } else {
        format!("{}::{}", l.module, l.key)
    }
}

/// Logical identity independent of the enclosing module: test fns inside a
/// `tests` module keep a `tests::` marker, test-support seams keep a
/// `test_support::` marker, everything else is its bare key.
fn logical_key(l: &Located) -> String {
    let last_mod = l.module.rsplit("::").next().unwrap_or("");
    match last_mod {
        "" => l.key.clone(),
        "tests" => format!("tests::{}", l.key),
        "test_support" => format!("test_support::{}", l.key),
        other => format!("{other}::{}", l.key),
    }
}

// ---------- normalisation ----------

struct StripVis;
impl VisitMut for StripVis {
    fn visit_visibility_mut(&mut self, v: &mut syn::Visibility) {
        if !matches!(v, syn::Visibility::Inherited) {
            *v = syn::Visibility::Inherited;
        }
    }
}

fn normalise_doc_text(text: &str) -> String {
    // De-link / re-path intra-doc links: `[`a::b::c`]` -> `c`,
    // `[text](a::b)` -> `text`, `[`x`][a::b]` -> `x`.
    let mut out = String::new();
    let chars: Vec<char> = text.chars().collect();
    let mut i = 0;
    while i < chars.len() {
        if chars[i] == '[' {
            if let Some(close) = chars[i + 1..].iter().position(|c| *c == ']') {
                let inner: String = chars[i + 1..i + 1 + close].iter().collect();
                let mut j = i + 1 + close + 1;
                // optional (target) or [target]
                if j < chars.len() && (chars[j] == '(' || chars[j] == '[') {
                    let closer = if chars[j] == '(' { ')' } else { ']' };
                    if let Some(end) = chars[j + 1..].iter().position(|c| *c == closer) {
                        j = j + 1 + end + 1;
                    }
                }
                let inner = inner.trim_matches('`');
                let last = inner.rsplit("::").next().unwrap_or(inner);
                out.push_str(last);
                i = j;
                continue;
            }
        }
        if chars[i] == '`' {
            i += 1;
            continue;
        }
        out.push(chars[i]);
        i += 1;
    }
    out
}

struct DocNorm {
    raw_docs: Vec<String>,
}
impl VisitMut for DocNorm {
    fn visit_attribute_mut(&mut self, attr: &mut Attribute) {
        if attr.path().is_ident("doc") {
            if let syn::Meta::NameValue(nv) = &mut attr.meta {
                if let syn::Expr::Lit(syn::ExprLit { lit: syn::Lit::Str(s), .. }) = &nv.value {
                    let raw = s.value();
                    self.raw_docs.push(raw.clone());
                    let norm = normalise_doc_text(&raw);
                    nv.value = syn::parse_quote!(#norm);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Flatten a token stream into comparable token strings; groups become
/// explicit open/close tokens so the sequence is unambiguous.
fn flatten(ts: TokenStream, out: &mut Vec<String>) {
    for tt in ts {
        match tt {
            TokenTree::Group(g) => {
                let (o, c) = match g.delimiter() {
                    Delimiter::Parenthesis => ("(", ")"),
                    Delimiter::Brace => ("{", "}"),
                    Delimiter::Bracket => ("[", "]"),
                    Delimiter::None => ("«", "»"),
                };
                out.push(o.to_string());
                flatten(g.stream(), out);
                out.push(c.to_string());
            }
            TokenTree::Punct(p) => out.push(p.as_char().to_string()),
            TokenTree::Ident(i) => out.push(i.to_string()),
            TokenTree::Literal(l) => out.push(l.to_string()),
        }
    }
}

/// Merge `:` `:` into `::` so path prefixes are recognisable.
fn merge_colons(tokens: Vec<String>) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut out: Vec<String> = Vec::with_capacity(tokens.len());
    for t in tokens {
        if t == ":" && out.last().map(|s| s == ":").unwrap_or(false) {
            out.pop();
            out.push("::".to_string());
        } else {
            out.push(t);
        }
    }
    out
}

fn is_ident(t: &str) -> bool {
    t.chars().next().map(|c| c.is_alphabetic() || c == '_').unwrap_or(false)
        && t.chars().all(|c| c.is_alphanumeric() || c == '_')
}

/// Drop every path-prefix segment (`ident ::` and a leading `::`).
/// Returns the reduced stream plus the list of removed prefix strings at
/// each surviving position (for reporting).
fn strip_path_prefixes(tokens: &[String]) -> (Vec<String>, Vec<String>) {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    let mut prefixes = Vec::new();
    let mut pending = String::new();
    let mut i = 0;
    while i < tokens.len() {
        if is_ident(&tokens[i]) && i + 1 < tokens.len() && tokens[i + 1] == "::" {
            // keep `<` generic turbofish `::<` separate: ident followed by `::` then `<`
            if i + 2 < tokens.len() && tokens[i + 2] == "<" {
                out.push(tokens[i].clone());
                prefixes.push(std::mem::take(&mut pending));
                i += 1;
                continue;
            }
            pending.push_str(&tokens[i]);
            pending.push_str("::");
            i += 2;
            continue;
        }
        if tokens[i] == "::" && pending.is_empty() && (i + 1 < tokens.len() && is_ident(&tokens[i + 1])) {
            pending.push_str("::");
            i += 1;
            continue;
        }
        out.push(tokens[i].clone());
        prefixes.push(std::mem::take(&mut pending));
        i += 1;
    }
    (out, prefixes)
}

struct Classified {
    categories: BTreeSet<u8>,
    details: Vec<String>,
    unclassifiable: Option<String>,
}

fn tokens_of(item: &Item) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut v = Vec::new();
    flatten(item.to_token_stream(), &mut v);
    merge_colons(v)
}

/// Apply the selected pure-move normalisations and return the token stream.
fn normalised(item: &Item, vis: bool, doc: bool, path: bool) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut copy = item.clone();
    if vis {
        StripVis.visit_item_mut(&mut copy);
    }
    if doc {
        DocNorm { raw_docs: vec![] }.visit_item_mut(&mut copy);
    }
    let tokens = tokens_of(&copy);
    if path {
        strip_path_prefixes(&tokens).0
    } else {
        tokens
    }
}

/// Visibility of the item itself, its named fields and its impl methods.
fn visibilities(item: &Item) -> Vec<(String, String)> {
    let text = |v: &syn::Visibility| match v {
        syn::Visibility::Inherited => "private".to_string(),
        v => v.to_token_stream().to_string().replace(' ', ""),
    };
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    match item {
        Item::Struct(s) => {
            out.push((format!("struct {}", s.ident), text(&s.vis)));
            for field in &s.fields {
                if let Some(ident) = &field.ident {
                    out.push((format!("field {ident}"), text(&field.vis)));
                }
            }
        }
        Item::Impl(i) => {
            for member in &i.items {
                if let syn::ImplItem::Fn(f) = member {
                    out.push((format!("method {}", f.sig.ident), text(&f.vis)));
                }
            }
        }
        Item::Fn(f) => out.push((format!("fn {}", f.sig.ident), text(&f.vis))),
        Item::Const(c) => out.push((format!("const {}", c.ident), text(&c.vis))),
        Item::Static(c) => out.push((format!("static {}", c.ident), text(&c.vis))),
        Item::Enum(e) => out.push((format!("enum {}", e.ident), text(&e.vis))),
        Item::Type(t) => out.push((format!("type {}", t.ident), text(&t.vis))),
        Item::Mod(m) => out.push((format!("mod {}", m.ident), text(&m.vis))),
        Item::Trait(t) => out.push((format!("trait {}", t.ident), text(&t.vis))),
        _ => {}
    }
    out
}

fn doc_strings(item: &Item) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut copy = item.clone();
    let mut docs = DocNorm { raw_docs: vec![] };
    docs.visit_item_mut(&mut copy);
    docs.raw_docs
}

fn classify(pre: &Item, post: &Item) -> Classified {
    let mut categories = BTreeSet::new();
    let mut details = Vec::new();
    if tokens_of(pre) == tokens_of(post) {
        categories.insert(0);
        return Classified { categories, details, unclassifiable: None };
    }
    let all_pre = normalised(pre, true, true, true);
    let all_post = normalised(post, true, true, true);
    if all_pre != all_post {
        let (lo, hi_a, hi_b) = first_diff(&all_pre, &all_post);
        return Classified {
            categories,
            details,
            unclassifiable: Some(format!(
                "token diff after all allowed normalisations @{lo}: pre `{}` vs post `{}`",
                all_pre[lo..hi_a.min(lo + 25)].join(" "),
                all_post[lo..hi_b.min(lo + 25)].join(" ")
            )),
        };
    }
    // (2) visibility: still different when every other category is normalised.
    if normalised(pre, false, true, true) != normalised(post, false, true, true) {
        categories.insert(2);
        for ((what, a), (_, b)) in visibilities(pre).iter().zip(visibilities(post).iter()) {
            if a != b {
                details.push(format!("vis {what}: {a} -> {b}"));
            }
        }
    }
    // (3) intra-doc link re-path / de-link.
    if normalised(pre, true, false, true) != normalised(post, true, false, true) {
        categories.insert(3);
        for (a, b) in doc_strings(pre).iter().zip(doc_strings(post).iter()) {
            if a != b {
                details.push(format!("doc {:?} -> {:?}", a.trim(), b.trim()));
            }
        }
    }
    // (1)/(4) path prefix, with test_support seam paths reported separately.
    let a = normalised(pre, true, true, false);
    let b = normalised(post, true, true, false);
    if a != b {
        let (sa, pa) = strip_path_prefixes(&a);
        let (_, pb) = strip_path_prefixes(&b);
        let mut pairs = BTreeSet::new();
        for (index, (x, y)) in pa.iter().zip(pb.iter()).enumerate() {
            if x != y {
                let segment = &sa[index];
                pairs.insert(format!("`{x}{segment}` -> `{y}{segment}`"));
                if x.contains("test_support::") || y.contains("test_support::") {
                    categories.insert(4);
                } else {
                    categories.insert(1);
                }
            }
        }
        for pair in pairs {
            details.push(format!("path {pair}"));
        }
    }
    Classified { categories, details, unclassifiable: None }
}

fn first_diff(a: &[String], b: &[String]) -> (usize, usize, usize) {
    let mut lo = 0;
    while lo < a.len() && lo < b.len() && a[lo] == b[lo] {
        lo += 1;
    }
    (lo, a.len(), b.len())
}

fn main() -> ExitCode {
    let args: Vec<String> = std::env::args().collect();
    match args.get(1).map(String::as_str) {
        Some("list") => {
            let (items, sha) = parse(&args[2]);
            println!("# sha256 {sha}");
            for l in items {
                println!(
                    "{}\t{}-{}\t{}",
                    full_key(&l),
                    l.first,
                    l.last,
                    if l.cfg_test { "test" } else { "prod" }
                );
            }
            ExitCode::SUCCESS
        }
        Some("audit") => audit(&args[2]),
        _ => {
            eprintln!("usage: moveaudit list <file> | audit <spec>");
            ExitCode::FAILURE
        }
    }
}

fn audit(spec_path: &str) -> ExitCode {
    let spec = fs::read_to_string(spec_path).expect("read spec");
    let mut pre_path = String::new();
    let mut posts: Vec<(String, String)> = Vec::new();
    let mut bases: BTreeMap<String, String> = BTreeMap::new();
    let mut moves: BTreeMap<String, String> = BTreeMap::new();
    for line in spec.lines() {
        let line = line.trim();
        if line.is_empty() || line.starts_with('#') {
            continue;
        }
        let mut parts = line.splitn(2, ' ');
        let kind = parts.next().unwrap_or("");
        let rest = parts.next().unwrap_or("").trim();
        match kind {
            "pre" => pre_path = rest.to_string(),
            "post" => {
                let (label, path) = rest.split_once(' ').expect("post <label> <path>");
                posts.push((label.to_string(), path.trim().to_string()));
            }
            "base" => {
                let (label, path) = rest.split_once(' ').expect("base <label> <path>");
                bases.insert(label.to_string(), path.trim().to_string());
            }
            "move" => {
                let (key, label) = rest.rsplit_once(' ').expect("move <key> <label>");
                moves.insert(key.trim().to_string(), label.to_string());
            }
            other => panic!("unknown spec line kind {other}"),
        }
    }
    let (pre_items, pre_sha) = parse(&pre_path);
    println!("input pre  {pre_path} sha256={pre_sha}");
    let mut post_items: Vec<(String, Located)> = Vec::new();
    let mut base_keys: BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, Vec<String>>> = BTreeMap::new();
    for (label, path) in &posts {
        let (items, sha) = parse(path);
        println!("input post {label} {path} sha256={sha}");
        for l in items {
            post_items.push((label.clone(), l));
        }
    }
    for (label, path) in &bases {
        let (items, sha) = parse(path);
        println!("input base {label} {path} sha256={sha}");
        let entry = base_keys.entry(label.clone()).or_default();
        for l in items {
            entry
                .entry(logical_key(&l))
                .or_default()
                .push(tokens_of(&l.item).join(" "));
        }
    }

    // Containers (modules) and `use` declarations are not audited as moved
    // items: modules are compared by their members, and `use` lists are
    // rewritten per destination file by construction.
    let auditable = |l: &Located| !l.key.starts_with("mod ") && !l.key.starts_with("use ");

    let mut duplicates = Vec::new();
    let mut unclassifiable = Vec::new();
    let mut mismatched = Vec::new();
    println!();
    println!("| item | old -> new | categories | detail |");
    println!("|---|---|---|---|");
    let mut pre_logical: BTreeSet<String> = BTreeSet::new();
    for pre in pre_items.iter().filter(|l| auditable(l)) {
        let lk = logical_key(pre);
        if !pre_logical.insert(lk.clone()) {
            unclassifiable.push(format!("{lk}: ambiguous duplicate key in pre file"));
            continue;
        }
        let pre_tokens = tokens_of(&pre.item).join(" ");
        // Candidate locations: same logical key in any post file, excluding
        // items that already existed byte-identically in that file's base.
        let found: Vec<&(String, Located)> = post_items
            .iter()
            .filter(|(label, l)| {
                if !auditable(l) || logical_key(l) != lk {
                    return false;
                }
                if let Some(base) = base_keys.get(label) {
                    if let Some(prior) = base.get(&lk) {
                        let now = tokens_of(&l.item).join(" ");
                        if prior.contains(&now) && now != pre_tokens {
                            return false;
                        }
                    }
                }
                true
            })
            .collect();
        if found.len() > 1 {
            duplicates.push(format!(
                "{lk}: {}",
                found
                    .iter()
                    .map(|(lab, l)| format!("{lab}:{}", l.first))
                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                    .join(", ")
            ));
            continue;
        }
        let Some((label, post)) = found.first() else {
            unclassifiable.push(format!("{lk}: not found in any post location"));
            continue;
        };
        if let Some(expected) = moves.get(&lk) {
            if expected != label {
                mismatched.push(format!("{lk}: expected {expected}, found {label}"));
            }
        } else if label != "runtime" {
            mismatched.push(format!("{lk}: not spec'd as moved but found in {label}"));
        }
        let c = classify(&pre.item, &post.item);
        let cats = c
            .categories
            .iter()
            .map(|c| format!("({c})"))
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join("");
        let moved = label.as_str() != "runtime";
        if let Some(reason) = &c.unclassifiable {
            unclassifiable.push(format!("{lk}: {reason}"));
        }
        if moved || !c.categories.contains(&0) || c.unclassifiable.is_some() {
            println!(
                "| `{}` | runtime.rs:{} -> {}:{} | {} | {} |",
                lk,
                pre.first,
                label,
                post.first,
                if c.unclassifiable.is_some() { "UNCLASSIFIABLE".to_string() } else { cats },
                c.details.join("; ")
            );
        }
    }
    for (k, label) in &moves {
        if !pre_logical.contains(k) {
            mismatched.push(format!("{k}: spec'd move to {label} but absent from pre"));
        }
    }
    // New (non-pre) auditable items in post files that were not in the base
    // snapshot either: report them so nothing is silently invented.
    let mut invented = Vec::new();
    for (label, l) in &post_items {
        if !auditable(l) {
            continue;
        }
        let lk = logical_key(l);
        if pre_logical.contains(&lk) {
            continue;
        }
        if base_keys.get(label).map(|b| b.contains_key(&lk)).unwrap_or(false) {
            continue;
        }
        invented.push(format!("{label}:{} {}", l.first, lk));
    }
    println!();
    println!("items found in more than one location: {}", duplicates.len());
    for d in &duplicates {
        println!("  DUP {d}");
    }
    println!("unclassifiable items: {}", unclassifiable.len() + invented.len());
    for u in &unclassifiable {
        println!("  UNCL {u}");
    }
    for u in &invented {
        println!("  UNCL new item not present before the move: {u}");
    }
    println!("destination mismatches: {}", mismatched.len());
    for m in &mismatched {
        println!("  MISMATCH {m}");
    }
    if duplicates.is_empty() && unclassifiable.is_empty() && invented.is_empty() && mismatched.is_empty() {
        println!("RESULT: PASS");
        ExitCode::SUCCESS
    } else {
        println!("RESULT: FAIL");
        ExitCode::FAILURE
    }
}
````

</details>

<details><summary>A.3 ACF-19 版 src/main.rs（sha256 e4bdccb75570f22193e4b0d04a2c508461dd4917a066efe023d02fd41db598b9，1079 行；新增 postmod 指令與 pure-move 類別 (5)）</summary>

````rust
//! ACF-17 scratch move-audit tool (ADR-ACF-10 "Move-audit method"),
//! extended for ACF-19 with pure-move category (5) and `postmod`.
//!
//! Usage:
//!   moveaudit list <file.rs>
//!   moveaudit audit <spec.txt>
//!
//! `list` prints every item (recursing into inline modules) as
//! `<module-path>::<key>\t<first_line>-<last_line>\t<cfg_test>`.
//!
//! `audit` reads a spec of lines:
//!   pre <path>                     -- the pre-move source (acf17-runtime-pre.rs)
//!   post <label> <path>            -- every post-move file that may hold a moved item
//!   postmod <label> <module> <path> -- a post file that is the body of an
//!                                     out-of-line `#[cfg(test)] #[path] mod
//!                                     <module>;` (its items get that module)
//!   base <label> <path>            -- the pre-move snapshot of an already existing post file
//!   move <pre-key> <label>         -- expected destination label of a moved item
//! and prints a per-item table plus the "found in more than one location"
//! and "unclassifiable" lists. The audit fails (exit 1) if either list is
//! non-empty, or if any spec'd destination does not match.
//!
//! Allowed difference categories (ADR-ACF-10 pure-move (1)-(4)):
//!   (1) path prefix: insertion/removal of leading `ident ::` path segments
//!   (2) visibility: any change of an item/field `Visibility`
//!   (3) intra-doc link: a `#[doc]` string that differs only after link
//!       normalisation (`[`a::b`]` / `[x](a::b)` -> last segment, de-link)
//!   (4) test_support seam path: a path-prefix difference whose path names
//!       a `test_support` segment
//!   (5) ACF-19 only: inside `ingest_agent_traces_payload_with_scope`,
//!       exactly the three inline SQL probes
//!       `conn.query_one_raw(Statement::from_sql_and_values(backend, SQL, ..))
//!        .await.context(CTX)` (the `sqlite_master` check and the two
//!       owner-claim SELECTs) may be replaced by calls to the new
//!       `capture::live` functions `agent_session_table_exists` /
//!       `scoped_owner_agent_kind`. Each substitution is verified and listed
//!       row by row: the new function's probe expression must be token-equal
//!       to the replaced one (same SQL literal; the owner-claim function takes
//!       its `.context(..)` text as the `context` argument and the call site
//!       must pass the identical literal), and the host's in-body
//!       `use sea_orm::{ConnectionTrait, Statement};` may be removed only
//!       when the post host names neither `Statement` nor `ConnectionTrait`
//!       (rustc's unused_imports lint, denied under clippy
//!       -D warnings, proves the replaced probes were its only users). The
//!       two new functions are accepted as new items only under this
//!       verification.
//! Category 0 = byte-identical token stream.

use std::{
    collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet},
    fs,
    process::ExitCode,
};

use proc_macro2::{Delimiter, TokenStream, TokenTree};
use quote::ToTokens;
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use syn::{visit_mut::VisitMut, Attribute, Item};

#[derive(Clone)]
struct Located {
    module: String,
    key: String,
    first: usize,
    last: usize,
    cfg_test: bool,
    item: Item,
}

fn is_cfg_test(attrs: &[Attribute]) -> bool {
    attrs.iter().any(|a| {
        a.path().is_ident("cfg") && a.meta.to_token_stream().to_string().replace(' ', "") == "cfg(test)"
    })
}

fn item_attrs(item: &Item) -> &[Attribute] {
    match item {
        Item::Const(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Enum(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::ExternCrate(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Fn(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::ForeignMod(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Impl(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Macro(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Mod(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Static(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Struct(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Trait(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::TraitAlias(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Type(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Union(i) => &i.attrs,
        Item::Use(i) => &i.attrs,
        _ => &[],
    }
}

fn type_key(ty: &syn::Type) -> String {
    ty.to_token_stream().to_string().replace(' ', "")
}

fn macro_statics(tokens: &TokenStream) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut names = Vec::new();
    let toks: Vec<TokenTree> = tokens.clone().into_iter().collect();
    for window in toks.windows(2) {
        if let (TokenTree::Ident(a), TokenTree::Ident(b)) = (&window[0], &window[1]) {
            if a == "static" {
                names.push(b.to_string());
            }
        }
    }
    names
}

fn item_key(item: &Item) -> String {
    match item {
        Item::Const(i) => format!("const {}", i.ident),
        Item::Enum(i) => format!("enum {}", i.ident),
        Item::Fn(i) => format!("fn {}", i.sig.ident),
        Item::Impl(i) => match &i.trait_ {
            Some((_, path, _)) => format!(
                "impl {} for {}",
                path.to_token_stream().to_string().replace(' ', ""),
                type_key(&i.self_ty)
            ),
            None => format!("impl {}", type_key(&i.self_ty)),
        },
        Item::Macro(i) => {
            let path = i.mac.path.to_token_stream().to_string().replace(' ', "");
            match &i.ident {
                Some(ident) => format!("macro_rules {ident}"),
                None => format!("macro {}!{{{}}}", path, macro_statics(&i.mac.tokens).join(",")),
            }
        }
        Item::Mod(i) => format!("mod {}", i.ident),
        Item::Static(i) => format!("static {}", i.ident),
        Item::Struct(i) => format!("struct {}", i.ident),
        Item::Trait(i) => format!("trait {}", i.ident),
        Item::Type(i) => format!("type {}", i.ident),
        Item::Union(i) => format!("union {}", i.ident),
        Item::Use(i) => format!("use {}", i.tree.to_token_stream().to_string().replace(' ', "")),
        other => format!("other {}", other.to_token_stream().to_string().chars().take(40).collect::<String>()),
    }
}

fn span_lines(item: &Item) -> (usize, usize) {
    use syn::spanned::Spanned;
    let mut first = item.span().start().line;
    for attr in item_attrs(item) {
        first = first.min(attr.span().start().line);
    }
    (first, item.span().end().line)
}

fn collect(items: &[Item], module: &str, inherited_test: bool, out: &mut Vec<Located>) {
    for item in items {
        let cfg_test = inherited_test || is_cfg_test(item_attrs(item));
        let key = item_key(item);
        let (first, last) = span_lines(item);
        out.push(Located {
            module: module.to_string(),
            key: key.clone(),
            first,
            last,
            cfg_test,
            item: item.clone(),
        });
        if let Item::Mod(m) = item {
            if let Some((_, nested)) = &m.content {
                let child = if module.is_empty() {
                    m.ident.to_string()
                } else {
                    format!("{module}::{}", m.ident)
                };
                collect(nested, &child, cfg_test, out);
            }
        }
    }
}

fn parse(path: &str) -> (Vec<Located>, String) {
    parse_in_module(path, "", false)
}

/// Parse a file whose items live in `module` (an out-of-line `#[path]`
/// module body); `cfg_test` marks the whole file as test-only.
fn parse_in_module(path: &str, module: &str, cfg_test: bool) -> (Vec<Located>, String) {
    let text = fs::read_to_string(path).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("read {path}: {e}"));
    let file = syn::parse_file(&text).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("parse {path}: {e}"));
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    collect(&file.items, module, cfg_test, &mut out);
    (out, sha256_hex(text.as_bytes()))
}

fn sha256_hex(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    let digest = Sha256::digest(bytes);
    digest.iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect()
}

fn full_key(l: &Located) -> String {
    if l.module.is_empty() {
        l.key.clone()
    } else {
        format!("{}::{}", l.module, l.key)
    }
}

/// Logical identity independent of the enclosing module: test fns inside a
/// `tests` module keep a `tests::` marker, test-support seams keep a
/// `test_support::` marker, everything else is its bare key.
fn logical_key(l: &Located) -> String {
    let last_mod = l.module.rsplit("::").next().unwrap_or("");
    match last_mod {
        "" => l.key.clone(),
        "tests" => format!("tests::{}", l.key),
        "test_support" => format!("test_support::{}", l.key),
        other => format!("{other}::{}", l.key),
    }
}

// ---------- normalisation ----------

struct StripVis;
impl VisitMut for StripVis {
    fn visit_visibility_mut(&mut self, v: &mut syn::Visibility) {
        if !matches!(v, syn::Visibility::Inherited) {
            *v = syn::Visibility::Inherited;
        }
    }
}

fn normalise_doc_text(text: &str) -> String {
    // De-link / re-path intra-doc links: `[`a::b::c`]` -> `c`,
    // `[text](a::b)` -> `text`, `[`x`][a::b]` -> `x`.
    let mut out = String::new();
    let chars: Vec<char> = text.chars().collect();
    let mut i = 0;
    while i < chars.len() {
        if chars[i] == '[' {
            if let Some(close) = chars[i + 1..].iter().position(|c| *c == ']') {
                let inner: String = chars[i + 1..i + 1 + close].iter().collect();
                let mut j = i + 1 + close + 1;
                // optional (target) or [target]
                if j < chars.len() && (chars[j] == '(' || chars[j] == '[') {
                    let closer = if chars[j] == '(' { ')' } else { ']' };
                    if let Some(end) = chars[j + 1..].iter().position(|c| *c == closer) {
                        j = j + 1 + end + 1;
                    }
                }
                let inner = inner.trim_matches('`');
                let last = inner.rsplit("::").next().unwrap_or(inner);
                out.push_str(last);
                i = j;
                continue;
            }
        }
        if chars[i] == '`' {
            i += 1;
            continue;
        }
        out.push(chars[i]);
        i += 1;
    }
    out
}

struct DocNorm {
    raw_docs: Vec<String>,
}
impl VisitMut for DocNorm {
    fn visit_attribute_mut(&mut self, attr: &mut Attribute) {
        if attr.path().is_ident("doc") {
            if let syn::Meta::NameValue(nv) = &mut attr.meta {
                if let syn::Expr::Lit(syn::ExprLit { lit: syn::Lit::Str(s), .. }) = &nv.value {
                    let raw = s.value();
                    self.raw_docs.push(raw.clone());
                    let norm = normalise_doc_text(&raw);
                    nv.value = syn::parse_quote!(#norm);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Flatten a token stream into comparable token strings; groups become
/// explicit open/close tokens so the sequence is unambiguous.
fn flatten(ts: TokenStream, out: &mut Vec<String>) {
    for tt in ts {
        match tt {
            TokenTree::Group(g) => {
                let (o, c) = match g.delimiter() {
                    Delimiter::Parenthesis => ("(", ")"),
                    Delimiter::Brace => ("{", "}"),
                    Delimiter::Bracket => ("[", "]"),
                    Delimiter::None => ("«", "»"),
                };
                out.push(o.to_string());
                flatten(g.stream(), out);
                out.push(c.to_string());
            }
            TokenTree::Punct(p) => out.push(p.as_char().to_string()),
            TokenTree::Ident(i) => out.push(i.to_string()),
            TokenTree::Literal(l) => out.push(l.to_string()),
        }
    }
}

/// Merge `:` `:` into `::` so path prefixes are recognisable.
fn merge_colons(tokens: Vec<String>) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut out: Vec<String> = Vec::with_capacity(tokens.len());
    for t in tokens {
        if t == ":" && out.last().map(|s| s == ":").unwrap_or(false) {
            out.pop();
            out.push("::".to_string());
        } else {
            out.push(t);
        }
    }
    out
}

fn is_ident(t: &str) -> bool {
    t.chars().next().map(|c| c.is_alphabetic() || c == '_').unwrap_or(false)
        && t.chars().all(|c| c.is_alphanumeric() || c == '_')
}

/// Drop every path-prefix segment (`ident ::` and a leading `::`).
/// Returns the reduced stream plus the list of removed prefix strings at
/// each surviving position (for reporting).
fn strip_path_prefixes(tokens: &[String]) -> (Vec<String>, Vec<String>) {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    let mut prefixes = Vec::new();
    let mut pending = String::new();
    let mut i = 0;
    while i < tokens.len() {
        if is_ident(&tokens[i]) && i + 1 < tokens.len() && tokens[i + 1] == "::" {
            // keep `<` generic turbofish `::<` separate: ident followed by `::` then `<`
            if i + 2 < tokens.len() && tokens[i + 2] == "<" {
                out.push(tokens[i].clone());
                prefixes.push(std::mem::take(&mut pending));
                i += 1;
                continue;
            }
            pending.push_str(&tokens[i]);
            pending.push_str("::");
            i += 2;
            continue;
        }
        if tokens[i] == "::" && pending.is_empty() && (i + 1 < tokens.len() && is_ident(&tokens[i + 1])) {
            pending.push_str("::");
            i += 1;
            continue;
        }
        out.push(tokens[i].clone());
        prefixes.push(std::mem::take(&mut pending));
        i += 1;
    }
    (out, prefixes)
}

struct Classified {
    categories: BTreeSet<u8>,
    details: Vec<String>,
    unclassifiable: Option<String>,
}

fn tokens_of(item: &Item) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut v = Vec::new();
    flatten(item.to_token_stream(), &mut v);
    merge_colons(v)
}

/// Apply the selected pure-move normalisations and return the token stream.
fn normalised(item: &Item, vis: bool, doc: bool, path: bool) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut copy = item.clone();
    if vis {
        StripVis.visit_item_mut(&mut copy);
    }
    if doc {
        DocNorm { raw_docs: vec![] }.visit_item_mut(&mut copy);
    }
    let tokens = tokens_of(&copy);
    if path {
        strip_path_prefixes(&tokens).0
    } else {
        tokens
    }
}

/// Visibility of the item itself, its named fields and its impl methods.
fn visibilities(item: &Item) -> Vec<(String, String)> {
    let text = |v: &syn::Visibility| match v {
        syn::Visibility::Inherited => "private".to_string(),
        v => v.to_token_stream().to_string().replace(' ', ""),
    };
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    match item {
        Item::Struct(s) => {
            out.push((format!("struct {}", s.ident), text(&s.vis)));
            for field in &s.fields {
                if let Some(ident) = &field.ident {
                    out.push((format!("field {ident}"), text(&field.vis)));
                }
            }
        }
        Item::Impl(i) => {
            for member in &i.items {
                if let syn::ImplItem::Fn(f) = member {
                    out.push((format!("method {}", f.sig.ident), text(&f.vis)));
                }
            }
        }
        Item::Fn(f) => out.push((format!("fn {}", f.sig.ident), text(&f.vis))),
        Item::Const(c) => out.push((format!("const {}", c.ident), text(&c.vis))),
        Item::Static(c) => out.push((format!("static {}", c.ident), text(&c.vis))),
        Item::Enum(e) => out.push((format!("enum {}", e.ident), text(&e.vis))),
        Item::Type(t) => out.push((format!("type {}", t.ident), text(&t.vis))),
        Item::Mod(m) => out.push((format!("mod {}", m.ident), text(&m.vis))),
        Item::Trait(t) => out.push((format!("trait {}", t.ident), text(&t.vis))),
        _ => {}
    }
    out
}

fn doc_strings(item: &Item) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut copy = item.clone();
    let mut docs = DocNorm { raw_docs: vec![] };
    docs.visit_item_mut(&mut copy);
    docs.raw_docs
}

// ---------- ADR-ACF-10 category (5): ACF-19 read-probe substitution ----------

const PROBE_HOST: &str = "fn ingest_agent_traces_payload_with_scope";
const PROBE_FNS: [&str; 2] = ["agent_session_table_exists", "scoped_owner_agent_kind"];
const PROBE_PLACEHOLDER: &str = "__acf19_probe_";

#[derive(Clone)]
struct PreProbe {
    sql: String,
    context: Option<String>,
    expr: syn::Expr,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
struct PostProbe {
    callee: String,
    context_arg: Option<String>,
    call: String,
}

#[derive(Default)]
struct ProbeReport {
    pre: Vec<PreProbe>,
    post: Vec<PostProbe>,
    use_rows: Vec<String>,
    problems: Vec<String>,
}

fn placeholder(n: usize) -> syn::Expr {
    let ident = syn::Ident::new(&format!("{PROBE_PLACEHOLDER}{n}"), proc_macro2::Span::call_site());
    syn::parse_quote!(#ident)
}

fn string_lit(expr: &syn::Expr) -> Option<String> {
    match expr {
        syn::Expr::Lit(syn::ExprLit { lit: syn::Lit::Str(s), .. }) => Some(s.value()),
        _ => None,
    }
}

/// Match `conn.query_one_raw(<path>::from_sql_and_values(B, SQL, V)).await
/// .context(CTX)` and return (SQL, CTX).
fn probe_shape(expr: &syn::Expr) -> Option<(String, syn::Expr)> {
    let syn::Expr::MethodCall(context) = expr else { return None };
    if context.method != "context" || context.args.len() != 1 {
        return None;
    }
    let syn::Expr::Await(awaited) = context.receiver.as_ref() else { return None };
    let syn::Expr::MethodCall(query) = awaited.base.as_ref() else { return None };
    if query.method != "query_one_raw" || query.args.len() != 1 {
        return None;
    }
    let syn::Expr::Call(statement) = &query.args[0] else { return None };
    let syn::Expr::Path(func) = statement.func.as_ref() else { return None };
    if func.path.segments.last().map(|s| s.ident != "from_sql_and_values").unwrap_or(true)
        || statement.args.len() != 3
    {
        return None;
    }
    Some((string_lit(&statement.args[1])?, context.args[0].clone()))
}

fn is_probe_sql(sql: &str) -> bool {
    sql.starts_with("SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table' AND name = 'agent_session'")
        || sql.starts_with("SELECT agent_kind FROM agent_session WHERE provider_session_id = ?")
}

fn is_placeholder_path(expr: &syn::Expr) -> Option<syn::ExprPath> {
    match expr {
        syn::Expr::Path(p)
            if p.path.segments.len() == 1
                && p.path.segments[0].ident.to_string().starts_with(PROBE_PLACEHOLDER) =>
        {
            Some(p.clone())
        }
        _ => None,
    }
}

struct PreProbeRewrite<'a> {
    report: &'a mut ProbeReport,
}
impl VisitMut for PreProbeRewrite<'_> {
    fn visit_expr_mut(&mut self, expr: &mut syn::Expr) {
        if let Some((sql, context)) = probe_shape(expr) {
            if is_probe_sql(&sql) {
                self.report.pre.push(PreProbe { sql, context: string_lit(&context), expr: expr.clone() });
                let ph = placeholder(self.report.pre.len());
                *expr = syn::parse_quote!(#ph.await);
                return;
            }
        }
        syn::visit_mut::visit_expr_mut(self, expr);
        // `async { PH.await }` is the replaced probe's own future wrapper.
        if let syn::Expr::Async(block) = expr {
            if block.capture.is_none() && block.block.stmts.len() == 1 {
                if let syn::Stmt::Expr(syn::Expr::Await(awaited), None) = &block.block.stmts[0] {
                    if let Some(path) = is_placeholder_path(&awaited.base) {
                        *expr = syn::Expr::Path(path);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

struct PostProbeRewrite<'a> {
    report: &'a mut ProbeReport,
}
impl VisitMut for PostProbeRewrite<'_> {
    fn visit_expr_mut(&mut self, expr: &mut syn::Expr) {
        if let syn::Expr::Call(call) = expr {
            if let syn::Expr::Path(func) = call.func.as_ref() {
                if let Some(last) = func.path.segments.last() {
                    let name = last.ident.to_string();
                    if PROBE_FNS.contains(&name.as_str()) {
                        self.report.post.push(PostProbe {
                            callee: name,
                            context_arg: call.args.last().and_then(string_lit),
                            call: expr.to_token_stream().to_string(),
                        });
                        *expr = placeholder(self.report.post.len());
                        return;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        syn::visit_mut::visit_expr_mut(self, expr);
    }
}

fn flatten_use(tree: &syn::UseTree, prefix: &str, out: &mut Vec<String>) {
    let join = |prefix: &str, ident: &dyn std::fmt::Display| {
        if prefix.is_empty() { ident.to_string() } else { format!("{prefix}::{ident}") }
    };
    match tree {
        syn::UseTree::Path(path) => flatten_use(&path.tree, &join(prefix, &path.ident), out),
        syn::UseTree::Name(name) => out.push(join(prefix, &name.ident)),
        syn::UseTree::Rename(rename) => out.push(format!("{} as {}", join(prefix, &rename.ident), rename.rename)),
        syn::UseTree::Glob(_) => out.push(join(prefix, &"*")),
        syn::UseTree::Group(group) => {
            for item in &group.items {
                flatten_use(item, prefix, out);
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Apply category (5) to both sides of the probe host and report each
/// substitution. Any deviation lands in `report.problems`.
fn probe_normalise(pre: &Item, post: &Item) -> (Item, Item, ProbeReport) {
    let mut report = ProbeReport::default();
    let mut pre_norm = pre.clone();
    let mut post_norm = post.clone();
    PreProbeRewrite { report: &mut report }.visit_item_mut(&mut pre_norm);
    PostProbeRewrite { report: &mut report }.visit_item_mut(&mut post_norm);
    if report.pre.len() != 3 || report.post.len() != 3 {
        report.problems.push(format!(
            "expected exactly 3 probe substitutions, found pre={} post={}",
            report.pre.len(),
            report.post.len()
        ));
    }
    // The host's in-body `use sea_orm::{ConnectionTrait, Statement};` served
    // only the replaced probes (`query_one_raw` / `Statement`); it may be
    // removed when the post host names neither and has no sea_orm import.
    if let (Item::Fn(pre_fn), Item::Fn(post_fn)) = (&mut pre_norm, &post_norm) {
        let probe_imports: BTreeSet<String> =
            ["sea_orm::ConnectionTrait".to_string(), "sea_orm::Statement".to_string()].into();
        let post_tokens = tokens_of(&Item::Fn(post_fn.clone()));
        let post_names_probe_import =
            post_tokens.iter().any(|t| t == "Statement" || t == "ConnectionTrait");
        let mut removed_rows = Vec::new();
        pre_fn.block.stmts.retain(|stmt| {
            if let syn::Stmt::Item(Item::Use(pre_use)) = stmt {
                let mut pre_paths = Vec::new();
                flatten_use(&pre_use.tree, "", &mut pre_paths);
                let pre_set: BTreeSet<String> = pre_paths.into_iter().collect();
                if !post_names_probe_import && pre_set == probe_imports {
                    removed_rows.push(format!(
                        "in-body `{}` removed (only the replaced probes used it)",
                        pre_use.to_token_stream()
                    ));
                    return false;
                }
            }
            true
        });
        report.use_rows.extend(removed_rows);
        let pre_tokens = tokens_of(&Item::Fn(pre_fn.clone()));
        if pre_tokens.iter().any(|t| t == "Statement" || t == "ConnectionTrait") {
            report
                .problems
                .push("pre host still names `Statement`/`ConnectionTrait` outside the replaced probes".to_string());
        }
    }
    (pre_norm, post_norm, report)
}

/// Canonical token text with every string literal replaced by its value, so
/// a re-indented `\`-continued SQL literal compares by its SQL text.
fn literal_value_tokens(expr: &syn::Expr) -> String {
    struct Canon;
    impl VisitMut for Canon {
        fn visit_lit_str_mut(&mut self, lit: &mut syn::LitStr) {
            *lit = syn::LitStr::new(&lit.value(), lit.span());
        }
    }
    let mut copy = expr.clone();
    Canon.visit_expr_mut(&mut copy);
    copy.to_token_stream().to_string()
}

/// The probe expression inside one of the new `capture::live` functions.
fn new_probe_body(item: &Item) -> Option<(String, syn::Expr, syn::Expr)> {
    struct Find(Option<(String, syn::Expr, syn::Expr)>);
    impl syn::visit::Visit<'_> for Find {
        fn visit_expr(&mut self, expr: &syn::Expr) {
            if self.0.is_none() {
                if let Some((sql, context)) = probe_shape(expr) {
                    self.0 = Some((sql, context, expr.clone()));
                    return;
                }
            }
            syn::visit::visit_expr(self, expr);
        }
    }
    let mut find = Find(None);
    syn::visit::Visit::visit_item(&mut find, item);
    find.0
}

/// Verify a new probe function against the replaced pre probes and the
/// call sites; returns the table detail or the reason it is unclassifiable.
fn verify_new_probe_fn(name: &str, item: &Item, report: &ProbeReport) -> Result<String, String> {
    let (sql, context, expr) =
        new_probe_body(item).ok_or_else(|| format!("{name}: no query_one_raw(..).await.context(..) probe"))?;
    let indices: Vec<usize> = report
        .post
        .iter()
        .enumerate()
        .filter(|(_, p)| p.callee == name)
        .map(|(i, _)| i)
        .collect();
    if indices.is_empty() {
        return Err(format!("{name}: never called from the probe host"));
    }
    let mut detail = Vec::new();
    for i in indices {
        let pre = report.pre.get(i).ok_or_else(|| format!("{name}: call #{} has no pre probe", i + 1))?;
        if pre.sql != sql {
            return Err(format!("{name}: SQL differs from replaced probe #{}", i + 1));
        }
        let mut expected = pre.expr.clone();
        if let syn::Expr::MethodCall(call) = &mut expected {
            match string_lit(&context) {
                Some(literal) => {
                    if pre.context.as_deref() != Some(literal.as_str()) {
                        return Err(format!("{name}: .context text differs from probe #{}", i + 1));
                    }
                }
                None => {
                    // `.context(context)`: the call site must pass the literal.
                    let site = report.post[i].context_arg.as_deref();
                    if site != pre.context.as_deref() {
                        return Err(format!(
                            "{name}: call #{} passes context {:?}, replaced probe used {:?}",
                            i + 1,
                            site,
                            pre.context
                        ));
                    }
                    call.args[0] = context.clone();
                }
            }
        }
        if literal_value_tokens(&expected) != literal_value_tokens(&expr) {
            return Err(format!("{name}: probe expression differs from replaced probe #{}", i + 1));
        }
        detail.push(format!(
            "probe #{} token-equal (string literals by value); SQL sha256 {}",
            i + 1,
            &sha256_hex(sql.as_bytes())[..16]
        ));
    }
    Ok(detail.join("; "))
}

fn classify(pre: &Item, post: &Item) -> Classified {
    let mut categories = BTreeSet::new();
    let mut details = Vec::new();
    if tokens_of(pre) == tokens_of(post) {
        categories.insert(0);
        return Classified { categories, details, unclassifiable: None };
    }
    let all_pre = normalised(pre, true, true, true);
    let all_post = normalised(post, true, true, true);
    if all_pre != all_post {
        let (lo, hi_a, hi_b) = first_diff(&all_pre, &all_post);
        return Classified {
            categories,
            details,
            unclassifiable: Some(format!(
                "token diff after all allowed normalisations @{lo}: pre `{}` vs post `{}`",
                all_pre[lo..hi_a.min(lo + 25)].join(" "),
                all_post[lo..hi_b.min(lo + 25)].join(" ")
            )),
        };
    }
    // (2) visibility: still different when every other category is normalised.
    if normalised(pre, false, true, true) != normalised(post, false, true, true) {
        categories.insert(2);
        for ((what, a), (_, b)) in visibilities(pre).iter().zip(visibilities(post).iter()) {
            if a != b {
                details.push(format!("vis {what}: {a} -> {b}"));
            }
        }
    }
    // (3) intra-doc link re-path / de-link.
    if normalised(pre, true, false, true) != normalised(post, true, false, true) {
        categories.insert(3);
        for (a, b) in doc_strings(pre).iter().zip(doc_strings(post).iter()) {
            if a != b {
                details.push(format!("doc {:?} -> {:?}", a.trim(), b.trim()));
            }
        }
    }
    // (1)/(4) path prefix, with test_support seam paths reported separately.
    let a = normalised(pre, true, true, false);
    let b = normalised(post, true, true, false);
    if a != b {
        let (sa, pa) = strip_path_prefixes(&a);
        let (_, pb) = strip_path_prefixes(&b);
        let mut pairs = BTreeSet::new();
        for (index, (x, y)) in pa.iter().zip(pb.iter()).enumerate() {
            if x != y {
                let segment = &sa[index];
                pairs.insert(format!("`{x}{segment}` -> `{y}{segment}`"));
                if x.contains("test_support::") || y.contains("test_support::") {
                    categories.insert(4);
                } else {
                    categories.insert(1);
                }
            }
        }
        for pair in pairs {
            details.push(format!("path {pair}"));
        }
    }
    Classified { categories, details, unclassifiable: None }
}

fn first_diff(a: &[String], b: &[String]) -> (usize, usize, usize) {
    let mut lo = 0;
    while lo < a.len() && lo < b.len() && a[lo] == b[lo] {
        lo += 1;
    }
    (lo, a.len(), b.len())
}

fn main() -> ExitCode {
    let args: Vec<String> = std::env::args().collect();
    match args.get(1).map(String::as_str) {
        Some("list") => {
            let (items, sha) = parse(&args[2]);
            println!("# sha256 {sha}");
            for l in items {
                println!(
                    "{}\t{}-{}\t{}",
                    full_key(&l),
                    l.first,
                    l.last,
                    if l.cfg_test { "test" } else { "prod" }
                );
            }
            ExitCode::SUCCESS
        }
        Some("audit") => audit(&args[2]),
        _ => {
            eprintln!("usage: moveaudit list <file> | audit <spec>");
            ExitCode::FAILURE
        }
    }
}

fn audit(spec_path: &str) -> ExitCode {
    let spec = fs::read_to_string(spec_path).expect("read spec");
    let mut pre_path = String::new();
    let mut posts: Vec<(String, String)> = Vec::new();
    let mut bases: BTreeMap<String, String> = BTreeMap::new();
    let mut moves: BTreeMap<String, String> = BTreeMap::new();
    let mut postmods: Vec<(String, String, String)> = Vec::new();
    for line in spec.lines() {
        let line = line.trim();
        if line.is_empty() || line.starts_with('#') {
            continue;
        }
        let mut parts = line.splitn(2, ' ');
        let kind = parts.next().unwrap_or("");
        let rest = parts.next().unwrap_or("").trim();
        match kind {
            "pre" => pre_path = rest.to_string(),
            "post" => {
                let (label, path) = rest.split_once(' ').expect("post <label> <path>");
                posts.push((label.to_string(), path.trim().to_string()));
            }
            "postmod" => {
                let mut fields = rest.splitn(3, ' ');
                let label = fields.next().expect("postmod <label> <module> <path>");
                let module = fields.next().expect("postmod <label> <module> <path>");
                let path = fields.next().expect("postmod <label> <module> <path>");
                postmods.push((label.to_string(), module.to_string(), path.trim().to_string()));
            }
            "base" => {
                let (label, path) = rest.split_once(' ').expect("base <label> <path>");
                bases.insert(label.to_string(), path.trim().to_string());
            }
            "move" => {
                let (key, label) = rest.rsplit_once(' ').expect("move <key> <label>");
                moves.insert(key.trim().to_string(), label.to_string());
            }
            other => panic!("unknown spec line kind {other}"),
        }
    }
    let (pre_items, pre_sha) = parse(&pre_path);
    println!("input pre  {pre_path} sha256={pre_sha}");
    let mut post_items: Vec<(String, Located)> = Vec::new();
    let mut base_keys: BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, Vec<String>>> = BTreeMap::new();
    for (label, path) in &posts {
        let (items, sha) = parse(path);
        println!("input post {label} {path} sha256={sha}");
        for l in items {
            post_items.push((label.clone(), l));
        }
    }
    for (label, module, path) in &postmods {
        let (items, sha) = parse_in_module(path, module, true);
        println!("input post {label} (mod {module}) {path} sha256={sha}");
        for l in items {
            post_items.push((label.clone(), l));
        }
    }
    for (label, path) in &bases {
        let (items, sha) = parse(path);
        println!("input base {label} {path} sha256={sha}");
        let entry = base_keys.entry(label.clone()).or_default();
        for l in items {
            entry
                .entry(logical_key(&l))
                .or_default()
                .push(tokens_of(&l.item).join(" "));
        }
    }

    // Containers (modules) and `use` declarations are not audited as moved
    // items: modules are compared by their members, and `use` lists are
    // rewritten per destination file by construction.
    let auditable = |l: &Located| !l.key.starts_with("mod ") && !l.key.starts_with("use ");

    let mut duplicates = Vec::new();
    let mut unclassifiable = Vec::new();
    let mut mismatched = Vec::new();
    println!();
    println!("| item | old -> new | categories | detail |");
    println!("|---|---|---|---|");
    let mut pre_logical: BTreeSet<String> = BTreeSet::new();
    let mut probe_report: Option<ProbeReport> = None;
    for pre in pre_items.iter().filter(|l| auditable(l)) {
        let lk = logical_key(pre);
        if !pre_logical.insert(lk.clone()) {
            unclassifiable.push(format!("{lk}: ambiguous duplicate key in pre file"));
            continue;
        }
        let pre_tokens = tokens_of(&pre.item).join(" ");
        // Candidate locations: same logical key in any post file, excluding
        // items that already existed byte-identically in that file's base.
        let found: Vec<&(String, Located)> = post_items
            .iter()
            .filter(|(label, l)| {
                if !auditable(l) || logical_key(l) != lk {
                    return false;
                }
                if let Some(base) = base_keys.get(label) {
                    if let Some(prior) = base.get(&lk) {
                        let now = tokens_of(&l.item).join(" ");
                        if prior.contains(&now) && now != pre_tokens {
                            return false;
                        }
                    }
                }
                true
            })
            .collect();
        if found.len() > 1 {
            duplicates.push(format!(
                "{lk}: {}",
                found
                    .iter()
                    .map(|(lab, l)| format!("{lab}:{}", l.first))
                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                    .join(", ")
            ));
            continue;
        }
        let Some((label, post)) = found.first() else {
            unclassifiable.push(format!("{lk}: not found in any post location"));
            continue;
        };
        if let Some(expected) = moves.get(&lk) {
            if expected != label {
                mismatched.push(format!("{lk}: expected {expected}, found {label}"));
            }
        } else if label != "runtime" {
            mismatched.push(format!("{lk}: not spec'd as moved but found in {label}"));
        }
        let mut c = if lk == PROBE_HOST {
            let (pre_norm, post_norm, report) = probe_normalise(&pre.item, &post.item);
            let mut c = classify(&pre_norm, &post_norm);
            if !report.pre.is_empty() || !report.post.is_empty() || !report.use_rows.is_empty() {
                c.categories.remove(&0);
                c.categories.insert(5);
                c.details.push(format!(
                    "{} probe substitutions + {} in-body import edit (see category (5) table)",
                    report.post.len(),
                    report.use_rows.len()
                ));
            }
            if !report.problems.is_empty() && c.unclassifiable.is_none() {
                c.unclassifiable = Some(report.problems.join("; "));
            }
            probe_report = Some(report);
            c
        } else {
            classify(&pre.item, &post.item)
        };
        if c.categories.is_empty() && c.unclassifiable.is_none() {
            c.categories.insert(0);
        }
        let cats = c
            .categories
            .iter()
            .map(|c| format!("({c})"))
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join("");
        let moved = label.as_str() != "runtime";
        if let Some(reason) = &c.unclassifiable {
            unclassifiable.push(format!("{lk}: {reason}"));
        }
        if moved || !c.categories.contains(&0) || c.unclassifiable.is_some() {
            println!(
                "| `{}` | runtime.rs:{} -> {}:{} | {} | {} |",
                lk,
                pre.first,
                label,
                post.first,
                if c.unclassifiable.is_some() { "UNCLASSIFIABLE".to_string() } else { cats },
                c.details.join("; ")
            );
        }
    }
    for (k, label) in &moves {
        if !pre_logical.contains(k) {
            mismatched.push(format!("{k}: spec'd move to {label} but absent from pre"));
        }
    }
    // New (non-pre) auditable items in post files that were not in the base
    // snapshot either: report them so nothing is silently invented.
    let mut invented = Vec::new();
    let mut new_probe_rows = Vec::new();
    for (label, l) in &post_items {
        if !auditable(l) {
            continue;
        }
        let lk = logical_key(l);
        if pre_logical.contains(&lk) {
            continue;
        }
        if base_keys.get(label).map(|b| b.contains_key(&lk)).unwrap_or(false) {
            continue;
        }
        let probe_fn = lk.strip_prefix("fn ").filter(|name| PROBE_FNS.contains(name));
        if let (Some(name), "live", Some(report)) = (probe_fn, label.as_str(), probe_report.as_ref()) {
            match verify_new_probe_fn(name, &l.item, report) {
                Ok(detail) => {
                    new_probe_rows.push(format!("| `{lk}` | (new) -> {label}:{} | (5) | {detail} |", l.first));
                    continue;
                }
                Err(reason) => {
                    invented.push(format!("{label}:{} {lk} ({reason})", l.first));
                    continue;
                }
            }
        }
        invented.push(format!("{label}:{} {}", l.first, lk));
    }
    for row in &new_probe_rows {
        println!("{row}");
    }
    if let Some(report) = &probe_report {
        println!();
        println!("category (5) probe substitutions in `{PROBE_HOST}`:");
        println!("| # | replaced probe (SQL head) | replaced `.context(..)` | replacement call | call-site context literal |");
        println!("|---|---|---|---|---|");
        for (i, pre) in report.pre.iter().enumerate() {
            let post = report.post.get(i);
            println!(
                "| {} | `{}` | {:?} | `{}` | {:?} |",
                i + 1,
                pre.sql.split_whitespace().take(8).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" "),
                pre.context.as_deref().unwrap_or("<non-literal>"),
                post.map(|p| p.call.as_str()).unwrap_or("<missing>"),
                post.and_then(|p| p.context_arg.as_deref()).unwrap_or("<in callee>")
            );
        }
        for row in &report.use_rows {
            println!("| import | {row} | | | |");
        }
        let expected_probe_fns = PROBE_FNS.len();
        if new_probe_rows.len() != expected_probe_fns {
            invented.push(format!(
                "expected {expected_probe_fns} verified new probe functions in capture::live, found {}",
                new_probe_rows.len()
            ));
        }
    }
    println!();
    println!("items found in more than one location: {}", duplicates.len());
    for d in &duplicates {
        println!("  DUP {d}");
    }
    println!("unclassifiable items: {}", unclassifiable.len() + invented.len());
    for u in &unclassifiable {
        println!("  UNCL {u}");
    }
    for u in &invented {
        println!("  UNCL new item not present before the move: {u}");
    }
    println!("destination mismatches: {}", mismatched.len());
    for m in &mismatched {
        println!("  MISMATCH {m}");
    }
    if duplicates.is_empty() && unclassifiable.is_empty() && invented.is_empty() && mismatched.is_empty() {
        println!("RESULT: PASS");
        ExitCode::SUCCESS
    } else {
        println!("RESULT: FAIL");
        ExitCode::FAILURE
    }
}
````

</details>
